      {
        "urn": "7363898855278477315",

          "date": "2025-08-20 13:45:01",

          "timestamp": 1755690301723
        },
        "text": "Toughness is killing your leadership.\nAnd your team can feel it.\n\nYou think pushing through shows strength.\n\nBut what it really shows… is fear.\n\nFear of slowing down.\nFear of being seen.\nFear of letting go.\n\nBut here’s the truth:\n\nresilience isn’t about pushing harder.\n\nIt’s about adapting smarter.\n\n💡 The best founders I work with don’t pride themselves on endurance.\n\nThey pride themselves on adjustment.\n\nThey:\n✅ Know when to push- and when to pause\n✅ Make space to think, not just act\n✅ Let go of what's not working, fast\n\nBecause resilience isn’t about showing strength.\n\nIt’s about staying flexible under pressure.\n\nThe most dangerous mindset for a CEO?\n\n“I just need to push through.”\n\nThat’s not leadership.\n\nThat’s how burnout wins.\n\n-------------------------------------------------------\n▶️ Follow me if you’re a CEO or founder scaling fast and refusing to burn out doing it.\n\n🧭 P.S. Subscribe to Self-Coaching for Leaders - my newsletter for growth-minded CEOs who want to lead with clarity, courage and control: https://lnkd.in/eT2MnRPC\n\n♻️ Repost if this feels like something your past self needed to hear.",

      {
        "urn": "7363540435048222720",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7363540305519804417",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-19 14:00:47",
          "relative": "23 hours ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1755604847681
        },
        "text": "What if your greatest leadership lessons came long before you ever had a title?\n\nFor Katie O'Malley, founder of Encourage Coaching, those lessons started at 10 years old - when her mother grounded her for talking about herself for an hour without once asking her best friend a question.\n\nThat moment of tough love shaped Katie into the leader she is today: one who listens deeply, leads with courage and builds human connection at the center of every role she’s held.\n\nFrom political campaigns… \nto nonprofit fundraising… \nto battling burnout in corporate real estate… \nto finally launching her own coaching practice at 40, \nKatie’s journey is proof that leadership is forged in resilience and reflection, not just ambition.\n\nLessons from Katie’s journey:\n\n➡️ Listening is leadership.\n\n➡️ Hardship fuels empathy.\n\n➡️ Courage means betting on yourself - especially when the timing feels risky.\n\n➡️ Independence is powerful, but asking for help is transformative.\n\n➡️ Legacy is built on generosity, not just achievements.\n\nKatie’s story is a reminder that the most powerful leadership doesn’t come from titles - it comes from how you show up, every single day.\n\n🎧 Listen to Katie’s episode here: https://lnkd.in/esXwdqbs\n\n----------------------\n🔔 Follow me for more bold leadership stories and self-coaching tools to help you lead from within.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-your-greatest-leadership-lessons-activity-7363540435048222720-e5eQ?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",

      {
        "urn": "7363166527392522240",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7363149460341673984",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-18 13:15:01",
          "relative": "2 days ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1755515701149
        },
        "text": "Most leaders chase productivity hacks.\nBut the ones who thrive do something very different.\n\nThey don’t just lead their teams.\nThey self-coach.\n\nAnd neuroscience backs this up: leaders who practice reflective self-coaching increase their emotional regulation and decision-making quality \n(Yale Center for Emotional Intelligence)\n\nHere’s what self-coached leaders do differently 👇\n\n1️⃣ They pause before reacting.\nResearch shows it takes just 90 seconds for an emotional trigger to pass through the brain’s chemical cycle.\nThat pause prevents regret-driven decisions.\n\n2️⃣ They get curious about emotion.\nHarvard studies link emotional curiosity to higher resilience.\nInstead of asking “How do I get rid of this feeling?”, they ask “What is this feeling teaching me?”\n\n3️⃣ They create brain-based routines.\nPeak performance isn’t about grinding harder.\nIt’s about micro-resets - short walks, breathing exercises, mindfulness breaks - that restore the prefrontal cortex and sharpen focus.\n\n4️⃣ They name what’s hard.\nAccording to Brené Brown’s research, naming emotions reduces their intensity.\nLanguage is self-regulation in action.\nWhat you can name, you can tame.\n\n5️⃣ They lead like humans, not machines.\nThe WHO predicts stress-related illness will be the leading cause of workplace absence by 2030.\nSelf-coached leaders know hustle ≠ growth.\nSustainability = growth.\n\nLesson: Self-coaching isn’t about perfection.\n\nIt’s about awareness, reflection and alignment with what matters most.\n\n----------------------------------------------------\n📌 Follow me for leadership insights, self-coaching tools and strategies to help you lead with clarity and impact.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_5-habits-of-self-coached-leaders-activity-7363166527392522240-rVNc?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",

      {
        "urn": "7362430426805596160",

        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-16 12:30:01",
          "relative": "4 days ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1755340201093
        },
        "text": "What if your brain is the bottleneck - not your strategy?\n\nThis week’s newsletter features neuroscience-based leadership coach Soraya Shaw 🧠 MSc on the emotional cost of high performance - and why we need to stop building companies on burned-out minds.\n\nInside:\n🧠 The neuroscience of stress, creativity and burnout\n\n💬 Why leaders confuse emotional suppression with resilience\n\n🔥 How self-coached CEOs protect their cognitive edge\n\nPlus:\n📜 A journal from Louise Otto-Peters on grief, courage and defying silence\n\n🎙 And a behind-the-scenes look at what really shapes powerful storytelling\n\n👉 Read the full edition and answer this:\n \nwhat emotion have you been suppressing to “stay strong” and what would change if you led with it instead?",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-your-brain-is-the-bottleneck-not-activity-7362430426805596160-5QJh?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {

        "urn": "7361724171074080769",

          "date": "2025-08-14 13:43:36",
          "relative": "6 days ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1755171816605
        },
        "text": "I’ve seen brilliant leaders burn out chasing every urgent fire.\n\nClarity is the antidote to overwhelm.\n \nIf you’re feeling stretched in every direction, you’re not alone.\n Here’s how to create space for what really matters:\n\nStop letting others’ priorities dictate your agenda\nGet crystal clear on your top three strategic goals\nBuild a weekly rhythm that protects thinking time\n \nWhen your focus is scattered, your impact is diluted.\n \nI’m offering a free discovery session where you’ll leave with your personal blueprint for solving this exact problem—tailored to your leadership reality. DM me or comment below BLUEPRINT if you’d like one.",

      {
        "urn": "7361713219708616708",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7361682336578445312",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-14 13:00:05",
          "relative": "6 days ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1755169205596
        },
        "text": "What if your first leadership test came at 16… running your late father’s business - with zero experience?\n\nFor Soraya Shaw 🧠 MSc, applied neuroscientist, executive coach and author, this wasn’t a career plan. It was survival.\n\nFrom those chaotic early years came a fiercely independent leader - one who’s since guided global clients to lead with both science and humanity.\n\nLessons from Soraya’s journey:\n➡️ Independence is forged, not gifted – hardship can be the ultimate leadership school.\n\n➡️ Curiosity is a lifelong asset – it fuels creativity, innovation and better thinking.\n\n➡️ Burnout hides in plain sight – it took her son’s words to trigger a complete career reinvention.\n\n➡️ Brains need care, too – leadership isn’t just about KPIs; it’s about brain health and human potential.\n\n➡️ Legacy is personal – her mission is to leave both a professional impact and a warm family memory.\n\nSoraya’s story is a rare blend of science, storytelling and survival - and a reminder that the brain is every leader’s greatest competitive advantage.\n\n👀 Watch the full episode: https://lnkd.in/ejrnJzn8\n\n----------------------------------\n🎧 Subscribe to Confessions of a Successful Leader YouTube channel: https://lnkd.in/epA9ttWA\n\n🔔 Follow me for more interviews, insights and self-coaching tools for leaders and founders building with purpose.",

      {
        "urn": "7361364252101615616",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7361364252101615616",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-13 13:53:25",
          "relative": "6 days ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1755086005235
        },
        "text": "Most leaders don’t change until something breaks.\n\nTheir team.\n\nTheir health.\n\nTheir marriage.\n\nTheir mind.\n\nBy then, it’s already expensive.\n\nHere’s the truth:\nIf you wait for a crisis to take control,\nyou’re not leading.\n\nYou’re reacting.\n\nThat might work when you're junior.\n\nBut as a founder or CEO? It’s dangerous.\n\nHere’s the shift:\n✅ Self-leadership gives you power in real time\n✅ It helps you act on patterns, not just problems\n✅ It creates the calm that others lean on\n\nI’ve seen it again and again:\nThe highest-performing founders don’t wait for clarity.\n\nThey create it-through self-leadership.\n\nBecause if you don’t steer the ship…\nyour fear, your stress or your past will.\n\nAnd that’s not leadership.\n\nThat’s survival.\n\n---------------------------------------------\n▶️ Follow me if you’re a CEO or founder scaling fast and refusing to burn out doing it.\n\n🧭 P.S. Subscribe to Self-Coaching for Leaders - my newsletter for growth-minded CEOs who want to lead with clarity, courage, and control: https://lnkd.in/eT2MnRPC\n\n♻️ Repost if this feels timely - for you or your network.",

          "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E22AQHay2xWvKZ8tA/feedshare-shrink_1280/B4EZijRxmUHoAk-/0/1755086004169?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=0um7xhc8ZPS94y_k9uFy3ZxkmKX1yRSNnNs3wznrK54",

      {
        "urn": "7361017821100748800",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7361017730130468864",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-12 14:56:49",
          "relative": "1 week ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1755003409648
        },
        "text": "What if your greatest leadership lesson came… before you even had a title?\n\nFor Satendra Singh, CEO and veteran leader, that moment happened at age 14- when his father told him, “now it’s up to you.”\n\nFrom that day forward, Satendra carried three principles into every role, every team, and every challenge:\n\n➡️ Own the responsibility – accountability starts the moment you decide it’s yours.\n\n➡️ Do what’s right – even if it’s harder, slower or less popular.\n\n➡️ Give people the chance to surprise you – they can achieve more than they believe.\n\nOver a career spanning decades and multiple CEO roles, Satendra has learned that leadership is about context - adapting to the people, the situation and the moment you’re in.\n\nHis story is a masterclass in moving from doing to delegating, from aggressive to assertive, and from leading projects to developing people.\n\n🎧 Listen to the full episode here: https://lnkd.in/dmvP-85x\n\n🔔 Follow me for more bold leadership stories and self-coaching tools that help leaders lead from within.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-your-greatest-leadership-lesson-came-activity-7361017821100748800-KGmc?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

      {
        "urn": "7360959424129040384",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7360959424129040384",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-12 11:04:46",
          "relative": "1 week ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1754989486725
        },
        "text": "I've done over 100 podcast interviews, and I’m still learning how to make each episode more meaningful, more useful, and more resonant.\n\nThat’s why I recently sat down with Kevin Redmond on The Podcasters Lab. It wasn’t just a chat, it was a chance to get a fresh set of eyes (and ears) on how I run Confessions of a Successful Leader.\n\nWe covered:\n\nHow trauma subtly shapes leadership, and how that affects the stories we tell\n\nWhat I could be doing differently to create more clarity and impact for listeners\n\nHow I might invite more of you, the people I want to serve, to engage, reflect, and take action\n\nIf you listen to the show, this episode gives you a peek behind the curtain, and a sense of the work I’m doing to make it even better.\n\nSpotify: https://lnkd.in/da6Kschj\n\nApple:https://lnkd.in/dJs8F79P",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_ive-done-over-100-podcast-interviews-and-activity-7360959424129040384-viui?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

      {
        "urn": "7360638747240980480",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7360638746133729280",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-11 13:50:31",
          "relative": "1 week ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1754913031397
        },
        "text": "Most leaders know what they do.\nSome know how they do it.\n\nBut very few can answer the one question that shapes everything:\n\nwhy am I here?\n\nWithout clarity of purpose, success often feels like running at full speed in the wrong direction.\n\nIn my coaching work, I’ve seen this question transform leaders from reactive to intentional.\n\nHere’s the truth:\nYour “why” isn’t found in a strategy meeting.\n\nIt’s uncovered through reflection, self-awareness, and courageous questioning.\n\nIn this carousel, I share 4 self-coaching techniques to help you uncover your purpose:\n\n1️⃣ Clarify your values\n2️⃣ Identify your energizers\n3️⃣ Imagine your legacy\n4️⃣ Use the 5-Why method to go deeper\n\n💡 If you want more clarity in your leadership, start here.\n\nThese tools work whether you’re leading a start-up or a global organisation.\n\nYour turn:\n\nWhat’s one word that captures your “why” right now?\n\nDrop it in the comments - it might inspire someone else.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_4-self-coaching-techniques-for-leadership-activity-7360638747240980480-Eenh?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",

        },

          "title": "4 self-coaching techniques for leadership purpose",
          "page_count": 7,
          "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/document/media/v2/D4E1FAQHLSobC6G9FaQ/feedshare-document-sanitized-pdf/B4EZiY9WRKGwBA-/0/1754912893672?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=YWyqnC_Fxh7_fMqgWsBCJu4KlqGCgilot-ZQpCjh_f0",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E1FAQHLSobC6G9FaQ/feedshare-document-cover-images_1920/B4EZiY9WRKGwA0-/0/1754912896507?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=X1Bb7eul1P2h7mJB94AUq4wcQz7Pt01ZYl0fJt6-HWc"
        }

      {
        "urn": "7359553887332098048",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7359553886098972672",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-08 13:59:40",
          "relative": "1 week ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1754654380639
        },
        "text": "Are you leading… or just performing leadership?\n\nThis week’s newsletter features Dominic Grinstead, CEO of MetLife UK, whose journey from mimicry to mastery reveals what happens when leaders stop playing the part - and start owning their voice.\n\nInside:\n\n🧠 Why copying other leaders is a ceiling disguised as strategy\n\n⚖️ How to shift leadership styles when your team needs clarity, not consensus\n\n🔥 A powerful lens for turning fear of failure into focused momentum\n\nPlus: a bold fictional journal from Victor Hugo on peace, pride and speaking uncomfortable truths - and a real look at the accountability gap no one talks about at the top.\n\n👉 Read the full edition below\n\nP.S. a question for you: where are you still performing - when you could be leading like you mean it?",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_are-you-leading-or-just-performing-leadership-activity-7359553887332098048-vfeF?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 6,
          "like": 6,
          "support": 0,
          "love": 0,
          "insight": 0,
          "celebrate": 0,
          "funny": 0,
          "comments": 4,
          "reposts": 1
        },
        "article": {
          "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/pulse/own-role-rewrite-rules-andrew-tallents-5z7de?trackingId=fTD0K1PrA1gHWIeJIrTcYw%3D%3D",
          "title": "Own the Role, Rewrite the Rules",
          "subtitle": "Andrew Tallents",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E12AQEn6vfDLQOTiw/article-cover_image-shrink_600_2000/B4EZiJb4M9GoAQ-/0/1754652446030?e=1761177600&v=beta&t=SeLLDHp_2UktPjYXWcTu0IVlUXQIgdSgi4VBAlczi1k"
        }

      {
        "urn": "7359168951601262592",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7359121903304814592",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-07 12:30:04",
          "relative": "1 week ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1754562604809
        },
        "text": "What if your leadership legacy wasn’t built on confidence… but on curiosity?\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, I sat down with Hendrik Lesser - award-winning game developer, entrepreneur, and “king of conflict-of-interest hats,” as he calls himself.\n\nFrom being nicknamed Alexander the Great at age five…\n\nTo building a values-driven company in a volatile industry…\n\nTo leading creative teams through ADHD, identity questions and burnout recovery…\n\nHendrik’s journey is raw, real, and radically human.\n\nLessons from Hendrik’s story:\n➡️ Clarity beats charisma: he learned to lead without being the loudest in the room.\n➡️ Moral compass > manual: leadership started with gut instinct - then got smarter with age.\n➡️ Invite, don’t chase: his mantra? “I don’t force people to work here. I invite them.”\n➡️ Don’t fake stability: he doesn’t pretend it’s easy - he just chooses to love it anyway.\n➡️ Curiosity is the constant: From history to RPGs to human nature, that’s been his edge from day one\n\nHendrik’s story isn’t about one big win. It’s about decades of reflection, reinvention, and real leadership in an industry where chaos is constant.\n\n🎧 Listen to the full episode: https://lnkd.in/dzEM_tru\n\n----------------------\n🔔 Follow me for more bold leadership stories and self-coaching tools for curious, purpose-driven leaders.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-your-leadership-legacy-wasnt-built-activity-7359168951601262592-H7Vi?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

        "media": {
          "type": "video",
          "url": "https://dms.licdn.com/playlist/vid/v2/D4D05AQGR4fP0WRYDtQ/mp4-720p-30fp-crf28/B4DZiDaYQGHYBw-/0/1754551396946?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=COtuWBw787K0_hqoAxGJVSsbRb0PmsCouQbTHtwxLO4",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4D05AQGR4fP0WRYDtQ/videocover-high/B4DZiDaYQGHYCI-/0/1754551394053?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=MV6FqQN10GVK9eU16L0UwNh52_bb4Qx8GenPgildbC8"

      {
        "urn": "7358834435431038976",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7358834434382438400",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-06 14:20:49",
          "relative": "1 week ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1754482849939
        },
        "text": "Here’s an uncomfortable truth:\nMost CEOs believe they’re great at accountability.\n\nBut in reality? They’re just great at working hard.\nThere’s a difference.\n\nHard work is not the same as being held to account.\n\nAnd self-accountability - without structure, feedback or friction - often turns into blind spots.\n\nWhat I see after 20+ years coaching senior leaders:\n\n✅ They hit targets.\n✅ They stay productive.\n❌ But they avoid the real work of being challenged.\n\nIt’s not a character flaw.\nIt’s a leadership trap.\n\nSo what does real accountability look like?\n👉 You create clarity - not just to-do lists.\n👉 You welcome feedback - not just results.\n👉 You embrace conflict - not just collaboration.\n\nAccountability is a mirror, not a scoreboard.\n\n🔧 Want to try something powerful?\n\nUse Marshall Goldsmith’s 6 Daily Questions for 7 days:\nDid I do my best to stay focused?\nBe fully present?\nManage my energy?\nBuild trust?\nBe kind - to others and myself?\nDelegate effectively?\n\nScore yourself.\n\nThen share those scores with someone you trust.\n\nIt’s confronting.\n\nBut that’s what real accountability feels like.\n\n👉 Swipe through the carousel. Save it. Reflect with your team.\n\n💬 YOUR TURN:\nWhat’s one habit you know you need to be held accountable for right now?",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_accountability-in-leadership-activity-7358834435431038976-2yoj?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",

          "page_count": 7,
          "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/document/media/v2/D4E1FAQH2SwFkuKywbQ/feedshare-document-chunked-pdf/B4EZh_TxK9GcBo-/0/1754482554554?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=WwIZY4ywStULTzAbpeLtm8yQxZ9siWuIV0sU48f9eOA",

        }

      {
        "urn": "7358451759486226433",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7358431694124654592",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-05 13:00:12",
          "relative": "2 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1754391612884
        },
        "text": "What if your biggest leadership shift didn’t come from a promotion - but a crisis that nearly broke you?\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, I sat down with Dominic Grinstead, CEO of MetLife UK and a quiet powerhouse of long-game leadership.\n\nFrom growing up with a multicultural family and overcoming dyslexia…\n\nTo leading through a career-defining regulatory crisis…\n\nDominic’s story is one of resilience, reflection, and redefining what it means to lead.\n\nKey lessons from Dominic’s journey:\n\n➡️ You don’t need to roar to lead. Quiet strength builds deep trust.\n➡️ Fear of failure can be fuel. If you manage it, not hide from it.\n➡️ Great leadership adapts. He went from democratic to directive - when his team needed it most.\n➡️ Long tenure doesn’t mean standing still. It means creating, reinventing, and growing again.\n➡️ Your toughest chapters often hold your best advice. \n\nDominic’s story is a masterclass in leading with both conviction and compassion - through change, through crisis, and through decades of impact.\n\n🎧 Watch and listen the full episode here: https://lnkd.in/e7Ftq9c7\n\n-----------------------\n🔔 Follow me for bold leadership stories and self-coaching tools for high-impact, human-centered leadership.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-your-biggest-leadership-shift-didn-activity-7358451759486226433-zHzh?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

      {
        "urn": "7358104454866370560",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7358104454866370560",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-04 14:00:09",
          "relative": "2 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1754308809010
        },
        "text": "If you’re not leading yourself, you’re leading from fear.\nSounds harsh.\n\nBut it’s true.\n\n👀 You make reactive decisions\n😤 You carry stress into every room\n🤐 You say yes when you mean no\n\nAnd it’s not just affecting you.\nIt's affecting your team.\n\nHere’s the shift:\nSelf-leadership isn’t soft.\nIt’s strategic.\n\n✔️ It creates mental clarity under pressure.\n✔️ It stops you outsourcing your confidence.\n✔️ It lets you lead from purpose, not panic.\n\nThe best CEOs I’ve coached?\n\nThey don’t just manage people.\nThey manage themselves - first.\n\nBecause your team can’t go deeper than you’re willing to go.\n\nAnd your culture can’t rise above your own self-awareness.\n\nSelf-leadership is not a luxury. It’s your edge.\n\nIn how you show up.\n\nIn what you tolerate.\n\nIn the way you make decisions that others avoid.\n\n---------------------------------\n▶️ Follow me, if you’re a founder or CEO building a high-performance business - without burning out.\n\n🧭 P.S. Subscribe to Self-Coaching for leaders - my newsletter for growth-minded CEOs who want to scale with clarity and courage: https://lnkd.in/eT2MnRPC\n\n♻️ Repost if this hits home for you - or for someone building at the edge.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_if-youre-not-leading-yourself-youre-leading-activity-7358104454866370560-65jZ?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",

      {
        "urn": "7357345027922010112",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7357345027234119680",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-02 11:42:27",
          "relative": "2 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1754127747517
        },
        "text": "If you want lions, stop throwing peanuts.\n\nThis week’s newsletter features Ashwin Bhadri - \nserial entrepreneur, operator of 7 ventures, and a leader who learned (the hard way) that micromanagement doesn’t scale - mentorship does.\n\nInside:\n\n🧠 How to evolve from gatekeeper to growth enabler\n\n💬 Why the best leaders build teams that outgrow them\n\n🔥 The hiring shift: choose passion over pedigree\n\nPlus:\n\n🔍 A fictional journal from Raja Ram Mohan Roy on leading through loss, reform, and moral courage\n\n🛑 And a hard truth about what silence in your leadership meetings is really costing you\n\nWhere are you still doing what someone else is ready to lead?\n\n👉 Read the full edition:",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_if-you-want-lions-stop-throwing-peanuts-activity-7357345027922010112-Dnbd?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 10,
          "like": 8,
          "support": 0,
          "love": 2,
          "insight": 0,
          "celebrate": 0,
          "funny": 0,
          "comments": 4,
          "reposts": 0
        },
        "article": {
          "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/pulse/build-room-step-back-andrew-tallents-3wp0e?trackingId=YAM6sV8xrAfq9GwZDXVGDw%3D%3D",
          "title": "Build the Room, Then Step Back",
          "subtitle": "Andrew Tallents",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E12AQErBjT0ndGRaA/article-cover_image-shrink_600_2000/B4EZhqHcsCHgAQ-/0/1754126996329?e=1761177600&v=beta&t=uPqGC_1cKR2l1nl4Lt1_maj74R_pnRopBzGFTre7drI"
        }

      {
        "urn": "7356994629663084546",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7356976756198711296",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-08-01 12:30:06",
          "relative": "2 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1754044206062
        },
        "text": "What if the best leaders aren’t the ones with all the answers… but the ones who learn how to let go?\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, I spoke with Ashwin Bhadri - serial entrepreneur, national advisor, and founder of 15 companies (and counting).\n\nFrom signing every check to leading 7 businesses across continents, Ashwin’s evolution is proof that scale starts with self-awareness.\n\n💡 Lessons from Ashwin’s journey:\n\n➡️ Micromanagement blocks scale - trust builds it\n\n➡️ The smartest hire? Someone who knows more than you\n\n➡️ Passion + profession = explosive performance\n\n➡️ Leadership isn’t about control - it’s about clarity\n\n➡️ Good people flourish when you get out of their way\n\nAshwin’s story is a masterclass in evolving from founder to multiplier - and building cultures where people want to lead.\n\nWatch and listen to the full episode here: https://lnkd.in/e54Ymqz4\n\n🔔 Follow me for more unfiltered leadership stories and self-coaching tools to help you lead from the inside out.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-the-best-leaders-arent-the-ones-activity-7356994629663084546-OvIM?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 15,

          "support": 0,
          "love": 2,
          "insight": 1,
          "celebrate": 0,

        },
        "media": {
          "type": "video",
          "url": "https://dms.licdn.com/playlist/vid/v2/D4E05AQHGhXvQpg4qwA/mp4-720p-30fp-crf28/B4EZhk7YR1HEBw-/0/1754039957809?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=C2Ltno-R5NBONIbi4hvJMmqpx1lachDrq6JvmTfjFXU",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E05AQHGhXvQpg4qwA/videocover-high/B4EZhk7YR1HECI-/0/1754039954930?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=8OP-lx9mWTJ5_ta0txffl7h-qLRTEBHpekm-tyJs5bc"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7356651538217345025",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7356651537273634817",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-31 13:46:46",
          "relative": "2 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1753962406687
        },
        "text": "Your ability to lead others is only as strong as your ability to lead yourself.\n\nEarly on, I poured energy into managing teams-yet progress stalled and morale dipped. \n\nThen I learned the truth: self-leadership is the bedrock of every high-performing team.\n\n(Swipe through the carousel to see why self-leadership beats any title)\n\nHere’s how to ramp up your self-leadership today:\n\n➡️ Reflect Daily: ask, “What mindset will best serve my team?”\n\n➡️ Invite Feedback: encourage honest insights on your leadership.\n\n➡️ Live Your Values: let core beliefs guide every choice.\n\nLead yourself with clarity-and watch your team follow with conviction.\n\nHow are you sharpening your self-leadership to elevate your team?\n\n🔔 Follow me for more self-coaching tools and leadership insights",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_importance-of-self-leadership-activity-7356651538217345025-tGYO?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 3,
          "like": 3,

          "love": 0,
          "insight": 0,
          "celebrate": 0,
          "funny": 0,

        },
        "document": {
          "title": "Importance of Self-leadership",
          "page_count": 6,
          "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/document/media/v2/D4E1FAQERt5Sx0Jzktw/feedshare-document-chunked-pdf/B4EZhgS5C5GcBo-/0/1753962226246?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=mP4frGyR2IyidMaEL5xW0M776TEEvvGRdUjuuO8zTnY",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E1FAQERt5Sx0Jzktw/feedshare-document-cover-images_1920/B4EZhgS5C5GcAw-/0/1753962227603?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=bnt6DSTNNARm99drcXwp2b05sAB0xDwuvwJ29Jmrz6A"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7356287458612809728",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7356287401167601664",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-30 13:40:03",
          "relative": "3 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1753875603345
        },
        "text": "What if the best preparation for corporate leadership… \nwas jumping off a sinking ship?\n\nThat’s where Alistair Dormer’s story begins - literally.\n\nFrom nearly drowning in naval training to becoming the first non-Japanese CEO of a Hitachi business, Alistair has led through warzones, boardrooms, and culture clashes with one unshakable belief:\n\nLeadership is earned through trust - not titles.\n\nLessons from Alistair’s journey:\n➡️ Be ready - or get ready: he passed the Navy’s swim test after 24 attempts\n\n➡️ True authority is shared: he learned to unlock a team’s talent by giving credit, not taking it\n\n➡️ Clarity wins cross-cultural trust: leading in Japan taught him that miscommunication is usually your responsibility\n\n➡️ Big wins require bigger humility: after landing a $10B deal, his CEO simply asked, “what’s next?”\n\n➡️ Know your strengths: his leadership edge? Building, not maintaining. Empowering, not controlling.\n\nThis conversation is a masterclass in scaling with empathy, leading across cultures, and showing up without ego - even when the stakes are sky-high.\n\n🎧 Watch and listen here: https://lnkd.in/eRSm6T7G\n\n-------------------------------------------------\n🔔 Follow me for more real leadership stories and self-coaching insights from those who’ve lived it.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-the-best-preparation-for-corporate-activity-7356287458612809728-AIKv?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

        "media": {
          "type": "video",
          "url": "https://dms.licdn.com/playlist/vid/v2/D4E05AQFMCZ1aUSJMcg/mp4-720p-30fp-crf28/B4EZhbIagEGoB4-/0/1753875600282?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=UvCtS776PS5Ojrif0HP1wXmky3k8WV4Iw-wSJJWaGgY",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E05AQFMCZ1aUSJMcg/videocover-high/B4EZhbIagEGoCQ-/0/1753875595150?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=xe6b_c6A9QVdsZj_MMCbrcSlNUmXTZy2L1nycW2vUO0"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7355930979984703489",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7355930979984703489",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-29 14:03:32",
          "relative": "3 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1753790612217
        },
        "text": "High-performing CEOs rarely fail because they do too little.\nThey fail because they do too much that doesn’t matter.\n\nI’ve been guilty of this myself.\n\nAsk yourself these two self-coaching questions:\n\n1️⃣ If I could only do one thing to live a successful life, what would it be?\n\n2️⃣ What are the 5–10 tasks that ONLY I can do?\n\nMy own list includes:\n✔️ Leading my team with clarity.\n✔️ Strengthening key board relationships.\n✔️ Protecting time for strategic thinking.\n✔️ Staying present with my family.\n✔️ Managing my personal wellbeing.\n\nEverything else?\n\nDelegated. Simplified. Eliminated.\n\n💡 Essentialism isn’t radical.\nIt’s leadership done right.\n\n👉 Comment 1 essential task that ONLY you can do in your business.\n I’ll share some reflections directly in the replies.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_high-performing-ceos-rarely-fail-because-activity-7355930979984703489-t2PD?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

      {

      {
        "urn": "7355560169981759493",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7355537253974089728",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-28 13:30:04",
          "relative": "3 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1753702204223
        },
        "text": "You don’t need a title to make an impact.\n\nEvery day, you’re already leading - \n\nthrough the choices you make, the energy you bring, and the trust you build. \n\nSwipe through the carousel to discover the subtle actions that define true leadership.\n\nThese aren’t checkboxes on a promotion form - they’re the habits that set exceptional leaders apart: \n\n✔️ stepping up first\n✔️ lifting others \n✔️ and navigating unseen dynamics with confidence.\n\nIf you see yourself in these pages, lean into it. \n\nYour leadership journey starts the moment you own your influence - no badge required.\n\n---------------------\n🔔 Follow me for more self-coaching strategies and insights that help CEOs and founders lead from clarity, not title.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_signs-youre-already-a-leader-activity-7355560169981759493-61zn?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 13,
          "like": 11,
          "support": 1,
          "love": 0,
          "insight": 0,
          "celebrate": 1,
          "funny": 0,
          "comments": 5,
          "reposts": 0
        },
        "document": {
          "title": "Signs you're already a leader",
          "page_count": 8,
          "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/document/media/v2/D4E1FAQEqPLiIVjaydA/feedshare-document-sanitized-pdf/B4EZhQbigMGUA8-/0/1753696058023?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=N1dwXusi2HX2Qi6m0R5RE_-21w-Jfbdtus698HDkbOA",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E1FAQEqPLiIVjaydA/feedshare-document-cover-images_1920/B4EZhQbigMGUAw-/0/1753696059684?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=p3Lhb0Ky0Mm44Nrk_1vq8xWcp2DeGdjZ9OcWtYxnKrM"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7355162294957318144",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7355162294957318144",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-27 11:09:03",
          "relative": "3 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1753607343425
        },
        "text": "Ever been in a meeting where no one speaks up?\nAnd everyone thinks they’re the only one holding back?\n\nI see this in executive teams every week.\n\nSilence is rarely strategy.\nIt’s usually fear in disguise.\n\nFear of being judged.\nFear of being wrong.\nFear of being exposed.\n\nLeaders don’t break teams by speaking.\nThey break them by staying quiet.\n\nThe real role of a leader?\nCreate a space where truth can breathe.\n\nSelf-leadership starts with:\n✔️ Noticing what you're not saying\n✔️ Asking what’s really going on\n✔️ Choosing courage over comfort\n\nYour presence creates permission.\nYour example invites honesty.\nYour silence speaks louder than words.\n\n🛠️ Want to build a team that speaks truth to power?\nWe run sessions that lays it all bare.\n\n💬 What helps others say the thing they’re scared to say?\n\n♻️ Repost if your team needs more truth and less fear.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_ever-been-in-a-meeting-where-no-one-speaks-activity-7355162294957318144-5PVB?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

      {
        "urn": "7354533839420989444",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7354533838720507907",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-25 17:31:47",
          "relative": "3 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1753457507949
        },
        "text": "If you’re still the engine of your business, you’re also its biggest bottleneck.\n\nThis week’s newsletter features Guy Wolstencroft - serial entrepreneur, Ironman, and founder of a national blood donation movement - on what founders get wrong about motivation, and how to lead from flow instead of force.\n\nInside:\n🚫 Why grit alone will burn you out\n💡 How to shift from proving yourself to empowering others\n🧭 What happens when you let go - and your team steps up\n\nPlus: a powerful fictional journal from King Ludwig I and a new space I’ve built to help leaders like you self-coach in real time.\n\nWhere are you still trying to drive the bus harder - when you should be letting others take the wheel?",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_if-youre-still-the-engine-of-your-business-activity-7354533839420989444-F1VE?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 6,
          "like": 6,
          "support": 0,
          "love": 0,
          "insight": 0,
          "celebrate": 0,
          "funny": 0,
          "comments": 2,
          "reposts": 1
        },
        "article": {
          "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/pulse/from-force-flow-redefining-how-we-lead-andrew-tallents-89m5e?trackingId=oehJ9AAWYA3m297Zjh%2BCrQ%3D%3D",
          "title": "From Force to Flow: Redefining How We Lead",
          "subtitle": "Andrew Tallents",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E12AQE5mXGF8NEL2A/article-cover_image-shrink_600_2000/B4EZhCMhamGwAQ-/0/1753457237295?e=1761177600&v=beta&t=yB5E_M7O1AQ0GzegTfKmPOuEYQmR8TQiITPVL5HgA4g"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7354527496526000128",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7354527496526000128",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-25 17:06:35",
          "relative": "3 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1753455995685
        },
        "text": "I have just finished reading an autobiography that made me think about my own life.\n\nIn My Life, David Jason doesn’t talk about success in terms of fame or fortune.\n\nHe talks about presence.\n\nAbout being grounded in the moment.\nAbout trusting the process.\nAbout enjoying the craft — not chasing the spotlight.\n\nIt’s easy for leaders to get caught in the future:\n🏆 The next win\n📈 The next milestone\n💼 The next title\n\nOr trapped in the past:\n😓 Regretting what they didn’t do\n🧠 Replaying what went wrong\n😠 Blaming others (or themselves)\n\nBut the real magic happens in the present.\nThat’s where self-leadership lives.\n\nShow up fully today.\nLead with intention now.\nLet the next thing come as it will.\n\nBecause true leadership is about how you lead yourself in this moment,\nnot who applauds you later.\n\n🎩 Lovely jubbly wisdom from a man who never tried to be famous — just real.\n\n💬 What helps others stay present when the pressure builds?\n\n♻️ Repost for someone in the thick of it today.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_i-have-just-finished-reading-an-autobiography-activity-7354527496526000128-qcOE?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

          "type": "image",
          "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E22AQFMr3cHcjm2Hw/feedshare-shrink_1280/B4EZhCHx0aHoAk-/0/1753455994069?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=W-DTzi5u1Chk9t0bzgYp5dEVKWmw7owFmLA4VE8IE4g",

      {
        "urn": "7354124219594539009",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7354124109770805248",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-24 14:24:06",
          "relative": "3 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1753359846972
        },
        "text": "What if your leadership superpower… came from never quite fitting in?\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, I sat down with Francesca Lagerberg, CEO of Baker Tilly International and one of the most respected voices in professional services.\n\nHer story isn’t about ticking boxes or climbing the ladder the “right” way.\n\nIt’s about navigating pressure, parenting, performance - and choosing to lead with purpose on her own terms.\n\nLessons from Francesca’s journey:\n➡️ Being the “extra” sibling taught her to find her own path\n➡️ Publishing gave her creativity-accounting gave her structure\n➡️ She didn’t build a career. She built a life-and a legacy\n➡️ Remote work isn’t a policy-it’s a trust signal\n➡️ The best leaders ask, listen, and keep learning-always\n\nFrom late-night writing to global leadership, Francesca’s journey is a masterclass in adaptability, self-awareness, and redefining what success really looks like.\n\n🎧 Listen to her story here: https://lnkd.in/et3cET9N\n\n🔔 Follow me for more real leadership stories and self-coaching tools for leading with impact.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-your-leadership-superpower-came-activity-7354124219594539009-jdtS?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 8,
          "like": 8,
          "support": 0,
          "love": 0,
          "insight": 0,
          "celebrate": 0,
          "funny": 0,

        },
        "media": {
          "type": "video",
          "url": "https://dms.licdn.com/playlist/vid/v2/D4E05AQHCLODCevb3WQ/mp4-720p-30fp-crf28/B4EZg8Y6ZVGUB8-/0/1753359842364?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=ldF8Dy22ditXJkz-lU5P1phC9oM4wKwwdX2SqzQt_6Y",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E05AQHCLODCevb3WQ/videocover-high/B4EZg8Y6ZVGUCU-/0/1753359835633?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=48FZDy-8AWGiqlGutgbBEjRL2O6RuYHR2-xRtEb6MBA"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7353756660122669057",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7353756660122669057",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-23 14:03:33",
          "relative": "3 weeks ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1753272213965
        },
        "text": "Early in my coaching career, I worked with hundreds of CEOs and chairs - but one thing I always saw was the same challenge:\n\nThey struggled to bridge the gap between their personal leadership growth and the dynamics of their teams.\n\nThe tension? Strong relationships make strong leadership.\n\nI quickly realised that as a CEO or chair, your relationship with your board and stakeholders is what shapes your success - whether you're leading a charity or a private equity-backed business.\n\n💡 But here’s the truth I wish all leaders knew sooner: \nInvesting in relationships doesn’t just enhance your leadership - it transforms your entire organization.\n\nI’ve worked with both charity CEOs and private equity leaders, and the difference is stark:\n\n🔵 In charity organizations, leaders prioritize mission-driven goals and the people who support them. The key? Building empathy and collaboration across diverse stakeholders.\n\n🔵 In private equity, the focus is on performance and results. But even here, trust and authenticity are what make or break a leader’s success.\n\nHere’s what I’ve learned and what I now coach leaders to do:\n✅ Embrace the power of relationships: Don’t just focus on performance. Build connections with your team, board, and stakeholders.\n\n✅ Create clarity in collaboration: Your relationship with your board isn’t about managing expectations; it’s about creating shared goals.\n\n✅ Be transparent: Whether in charity or private equity, your impact is defined by trust. Lead with openness.\n\n✅ Own your leadership story: Great leaders don’t wait for permission - they build trust by leading authentically.\n\n✅ Shift from transactional to strategic: Elevate your leadership by investing in relationships that go beyond the surface level.\n\nLeadership isn’t just about your output - it’s about how you show up and invest in the connections that drive collective success.\n\nIf you’re a CEO or chair, now’s the time to reflect on how you’re investing in your relationships. It’s what will truly elevate your leadership and drive sustainable growth.\n\nLet’s talk about how to take your leadership to the next level.\n\n------\n🔔 Follow me for more leadership insights, self-coaching tools, and strategies to build lasting connections that make a real impact.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_early-in-my-coaching-career-i-worked-with-activity-7353756660122669057-_r_q?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",

              "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E22AQHERcaYyxOs5g/feedshare-shrink_1280/B4EZg3KtMoGUAk-/0/1753272212601?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=5hJSZON02pLBS_ZBjxuBlyTinM98yXtlm4ZVhuGQ8uo",

      {
        "urn": "7353397047078572033",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7353396951435935745",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-22 14:14:35",
          "relative": "0 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1753186475534
        },
        "text": "What if your biggest leadership advantage… was being told you’d never make it?\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, I sat down with Guy Wolstencroft - entrepreneur, endurance athlete, proud father, and founder of six businesses (and counting).\n\nGuy’s leadership story didn’t start in a boardroom.\n\nIt started with an absent father, a powerhouse mother, and a decision:\nTo become everything he wished he’d had.\n\nLessons from Guy’s journey:\n➡️ Start at 60%-you’ll never be “ready”\n➡️ Adversity fuels ambition-but empathy builds legacy\n➡️ What’s the best that can happen? should be your mantra\n➡️ Real success is showing up-with your whole self\n➡️ Even Ironman finish lines mean more when you give the medal away\n\nWhether he’s building a business, cycling the Tour de France, or launching a global AI venture, Guy leads with optimism, grit, and radical clarity of purpose.\n\nThis episode is full of lessons for anyone building against the odds - and leading with intention.\n\n🎧 watch and listen here: https://lnkd.in/e5Q_4t4n\n\n-----\n🔔 Follow me for real leadership stories + self-coaching tools that help you lead from the inside out.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-your-biggest-leadership-advantage-activity-7353397047078572033-CCE8?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 5,
          "like": 4,
          "support": 1,
          "love": 0,
          "insight": 0,
          "celebrate": 0,
          "funny": 0,
          "comments": 4,
          "reposts": 1
        },
        "media": {
          "type": "video",
          "url": "https://dms.licdn.com/playlist/vid/v2/D4E05AQHi_4-C0kZVfg/mp4-720p-30fp-crf28/B4EZgyDkMtHgB0-/0/1753186467511?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=IVe5w-pN6PB98ZwUIPqf8ILmFBmkuSWtXhHX68PNeYs",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E05AQHi_4-C0kZVfg/videocover-high/B4EZgyDkMtHgCM-/0/1753186463882?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=pcxqiJWrdLaTDUkZR337dVouxUbXgICDQmna4QNBtLw"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7353045167882264577",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7353045166825402368",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-21 14:56:20",
          "relative": "0 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1753102580996
        },
        "text": "Early in my leadership career, I was outwardly confident - but internally, I second-guessed every move.\n\nI told myself I had to prove my worth. Work harder. Be smarter. Never show doubt.\n\nBut here’s the truth I wish I’d learned sooner:\nself-doubt doesn’t make you weak. It makes you human.\n\nAnd authenticity - not perfection - is what builds true leadership.\n\nThe moment I started owning my story, everything shifted.\n\nInstead of hiding what made me different, I leaned into it.\nI stopped striving for a version of leadership that wasn’t mine - and started building trust through shared purpose, clarity, and presence.\n\nHere’s what helped me - and what I now teach other leaders to do:\n✅ Embrace your background as a source of strength\n✅ Build trust by aligning goals, not controlling outcomes\n✅ Reduce imposter feelings through self-awareness\n✅ Show up authentically to inspire authenticity in others\n✅ Challenge negative self-talk with evidence of impact\n✅ Lead with flexibility - and watch innovation grow\n\nYour lived experience is not a liability. It’s your leadership edge.\n\nWhen you stop trying to “fix” yourself and start leading from who you truly are: you don’t just overcome imposter syndrome.\n\nYou become the leader others want to follow.\n—\nIf you’re a CEO, founder, or leader who’s tired of pretending to have it all together - this is your sign to do the real work.\n\nThe inner work.\n----\n🔔 Follow me for more practical self-coaching tools, leadership insights, and conversations that help you lead from clarity, not fear.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_overcome-imposter-syndrome-and-lead-activity-7353045167882264577--V2f?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

        "document": {
          "title": "Overcome Imposter Syndrome and Lead",
          "page_count": 6,
          "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/document/media/v2/D4E1FAQGFgmA13con6w/feedshare-document-url-metadata-scrapper-pdf/B4EZgtC9bfHgA0-/0/1753102420844?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=CIn-iLrY5wlPXF7wB42JprnEccZMghoRyTKqfKlnovc",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E1FAQGFgmA13con6w/feedshare-document-cover-images_1920/B4EZgtC9bfHgAw-/0/1753102421594?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=0d9qXMFbDaDzXg0OXhvDIefhkBj65UJhpi6PGvl1DNU"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7351964869702610945",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7351964868981231619",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-18 15:23:37",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1752845017839
        },
        "text": "Control may have built your business - \nbut it won’t grow it.\n\nThis week’s newsletter features Rich Goldfarb, former media exec and creative soul, on how grief, ego, and the need to \"do it all\" quietly shaped his leadership - and what changed when he let go.\n\nInside:\n- Why trying to clone yourself kills innovation\n- How childhood patterns shape founder behavior\n- The leadership shift from control to collaboration\n\nPlus: a powerful reflection on why many founders burn out after things start working - and how to rewrite the story that’s holding you back.\n\nP.S.: where are you still proving yourself - when you could be leading differently?",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_control-may-have-built-your-business-but-activity-7351964869702610945-lJOk?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

        "text": "The next Walkabout Wirral is on Thursday 24th July at 5pm. \n\nIf you work in Merseyside or are visiting the Wirral that day and would like to come along for a one hour walk around the lake and along the Wirral Way then please sign up here\n\nhttps://lnkd.in/eYWd-39T\n\nFor those who want to join us later we will be quenching our thirsts in the West Kirby Tap from 6pm onwards.\n\nGreat things are already happening as a result of this group walking together every month.             "timestamp": 1749841882281
          },
          "text": "I am excited to let you know that our second Walkabout Wirral, the newest group formed from the original Walkabout London, founded by Ben Brabyn is on the move again on the 26th June.\n\nWalkabout Wirral brings together leaders of all ages who want to think more clearly, connect more deeply, and act more decisively—simply by walking together. It’s about fresh air, open minds, and powerful conversations.\n\nMeeting on the 4th Thursday of every month, our second Walkabout Wirral is at 5pm on June 26th in West Kirby. If you work in Merseyside or are visiting the Wirral that day and would like to come along for a one hour walk around the lake and along the Wirral Way then please sign up here\n\nhttps://lnkd.in/eYWd-39T\n\nFor those who want to join us later we will be quenching our thirsts in the West Kirby Tap from 6pm onwards.\n\nI know that great things will happen as a result of this group walking together every month. Are you ready?",

      {
        "urn": "7351574370571493378",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7351574255483908097",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-17 13:31:55",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1752751915591
        },
        "text": "What if the thing that made you successful… \nwas also what stopped you from healing?\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, I sat down with Rich Goldfarb - media executive, former touring musician, and father of triplets - whose leadership journey began the day he lost his father at just 12 years old.\n\nBut what he shares in this conversation is more than a leadership story.\n\nIt’s a story about legacy, pressure, identity, and the cost of control.\n\nPowerful lessons from Rich’s journey:\n➡️ Stoicism can look like strength - but feel like survival\n➡️ Compartmentalization helped him lead - but also delayed healing\n➡️ At first, he hired clones. Later, he built teams that could outshine him\n➡️ He chased control until he learned to trust and delegate\n➡️ His true legacy? Developing talent and raising three grounded sons\n\nRich reminds us: Leadership isn’t just about success - it’s about self-awareness.\n\nAnd real growth doesn’t always come from drive. Sometimes it comes from letting go.\n\n🎧 Listen to the full episode here: https://lnkd.in/eqBYmVNr\n\n----\n🔔 Follow me for more raw leadership stories and self-coaching tools that help leaders lead from within.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-the-thing-that-made-you-successful-activity-7351574370571493378-YSF3?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

          "type": "video",
          "url": "https://dms.licdn.com/playlist/vid/v2/D4E05AQE35c_hDWPlXw/mp4-720p-30fp-crf28/B4EZgYJ1VDGYB8-/0/1752751912514?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=nIsHs_L8diaUgVA37hw3mkOa-LVlnO9CEPFD3rENeD0",

      {
        "urn": "7351126871671029763",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7351126871671029763",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-16 07:53:43",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1752645223539
        },
        "text": "“Why is my executive team so dysfunctional?”\n\nThat’s the question every Founder and CEO has asked me—when the team can’t decide, stalls, or falls into emotional re-enactments rooted in past patterns  .\n\nJerry Colonna  explains that dysfunction isn't about intelligence or intent. (Read more here https://lnkd.in/dhK2im-j)\n\n🔍 It’s about unconscious roles people slip into:\n\nThe Hero who always saves the day\n\nThe Cynic who critiques but never supports\n\nThe Ghost who stays silent to avoid conflict  \n\nThese roles aren’t random—they’re stories teams are unknowingly acting out, shaped by past pain and protective reflexes  .\n\n🤔 What if the real work isn't swapping people, but surfacing the story?\n\nIdentify patterns that keep repeating\n\nName the hidden roles in your team\n\nHold space for the messy, human dynamics\n\nWrite a new script together\n\nUntil these unconscious narratives are revealed, no re-org or off-site will truly shift the dynamic  .\n\n🚀 Ready to level up your executive team?\n\nContact us to run a systemic team coaching session for you that:\n\n1. Maps your emotional roles\n\n2. Surfaces the hidden narratives\n\n3. Co-creates new ways of working — with compassion and accountability\n\n✔️ Let’s replace unconscious patterns with collective clarity.\n\n📩 DM me or visit\n https://lnkd.in/dHZ2gc7r\n\nRepost if there's a team you believe deserves this level of clarity — and transformation.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_why-is-my-executive-team-so-dysfunctional-activity-7351126871671029763-GjB_?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

      {
        "urn": "7350852908373999616",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7350173833753976833",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-15 13:45:05",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1752579905599
        },
        "text": "What if your legacy was shaped not by what you built - but by what nearly broke you?\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, I sat down with Lotus (Sarah/Sharky) Harkness, and mother of three - whose leadership journey has been anything but linear.\n\nFrom the highs of go-to-market success in Silicon Valley tech…\n\nto 26 surgeries, a coma, and rebuilding everything from the ground up…\n\nLotus’ story is a lesson in radical resilience, reinvention, and choosing to lead with purpose - even when life tries to take you out.\n\nLessons from Lotus’ journey:\n\n➡️ Cheeky audacity is a leadership trait - not a flaw.\n\n➡️ Creative minds are often underestimated - until they lead.\n\n➡️ Real leaders feel deeply and still choose to rise.\n\n➡️ You can be both mother and founder - and not drop the ball.\n\n➡️ Legacy isn’t what you leave behind. It’s what you live through.\n\nHer story is wild, raw, and real - and a reminder that courage doesn’t always roar. \n\nSometimes it quietly says, \"I’m still here.\"\n\n🎧 Listen to Lotus’ episode here: https://lnkd.in/eYPMH7aj\n\n------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n🔔 Follow me for more bold leadership stories, real-life reinventions, and self-coaching tools that help leaders lead from within.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-your-legacy-was-shaped-not-by-what-activity-7350852908373999616-m-aj?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

          "type": "video",

          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E05AQHYs3aybeqrUg/videocover-high/B4EZgEQKC9GYCI-/0/1752418015541?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=iu28VuCMi2GMQ3YvpFYSWAo_4oGEsdApVcmq1_0FWNE"
