      {
        "urn": "7350533670220226561",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7350533670220226561",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-14 16:36:33",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1752503793292
        },
        "text": "Founders don’t fail from lack of vision.\n They fail from self-doubt, hidden beliefs, and burnout.\n\nThe strategy is there.\nThe market is ready.\nThe team is watching.\n\nBut the Founder?\n Still trying to control everything.\n Still scared of not being good enough.\n Still stuck in perfection or people-pleasing.\n\nI’ve coached hundreds of Founders.\n \nThe pattern is always the same:\n👉 They build the business.\n👉 Then they become the bottleneck.\n👉 Then they suffer in silence.\n\nAnd they don’t need a new tool.\n They need a new way of seeing themselves.\n\nThe only person stopping you\n living your dreams is you.\n\nThat truth hurts.\n But it also heals.\n\n💭 What helps others get out of their own way?\n\n♻️ Repost if this might help another Founder today.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_founders-dont-fail-from-lack-of-vision-activity-7350533670220226561-7z5e?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

      {
        "urn": "7350494277036072961",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7350125292826304513",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-14 14:00:01",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1752494401225
        },
        "text": "If you’re hiding your story to fit in, you’re shrinking the impact you were built to make.\n\nThis week’s newsletter features Sarah Walker-Smith - CEO, changemaker, and the first woman and non-lawyer to lead her firm - on why vulnerability isn’t a weakness in leadership. It’s the advantage.\n\nInside:\n🧠 Why your story builds more trust than your CV\n\n💬 How to lead with empathy across generations\n\n🎯 What it really takes to lead beyond performance and into purpose\n\nIf you’ve ever felt like you had to “prove” your way into the room, this is your reminder: you belong when you lead as you are.\n\nRead the full edition 👇",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_if-youre-hiding-your-story-to-fit-in-you-activity-7350494277036072961-2WkO?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {

          "support": 5,
          "love": 8,
          "insight": 2,
          "celebrate": 3,
          "funny": 0,
          "comments": 7,
          "reposts": 0
        },
        "article": {
          "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/pulse/from-reaction-recognition-andrew-tallents-mfyme?trackingId=weB2QWSqZO%2FraW9Mr%2BUwdQ%3D%3D",
          "title": "From Reaction to Recognition",
          "subtitle": "Andrew Tallents",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E12AQEnT_gWyht1nQ/article-cover_image-shrink_720_1280/B4EZgDjNjTH4AI-/0/1752406220974?e=1761177600&v=beta&t=h3VtR_uwLqaq34I5yYBGlHSysYVFHaznOd2F6R3O228"

      {
        "urn": "7349063235876073473",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7349063113951932417",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-10 15:13:34",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1752153214425
        },
        "text": "What if your biggest leadership strength… \ncame from the moments you felt most alone?\n\nWhen Alex Rodriguez was 7 years old, a teacher told him he’d never amount to much because of his stutter.\n\nWhen he was 18, a congressman looked him in the eye and blocked his dream of becoming an Air Force pilot.\n\nWhen he hit rock bottom in his twenties, he made a decision-to rebuild.\n\nToday, Alex is the CEO of DCG Public Affairs, a martial artist of 50+ years, a mentor, and a force for purpose-led leadership.\n\nLessons from Alex’s extraordinary journey:\n➡️ Service Is Strength: he doesn’t chase clients - he serves people whose values align.\n\n➡️ Meditation and Martial Arts Build Presence: his practice grounds how he shows up as a leader and father.\n\n➡️ Character Over Credentials: he once walked out of a meeting because someone judged him for not having a degree.\n\n➡️ Fail Big, Rise Bigger: his motto? Fall seven times, get up eight-and be proud of the scars.\n\n➡️ Leadership Is Legacy: it’s not the wins, but the impact we leave in people’s lives that matters most.\n\nAlex’s story is a reminder: \n\nLeadership is not what you inherit. \n\nIt’s what you build - especially when no one is watching.\n\n🎧 Listen to the full episode here: https://lnkd.in/dxSp7nEj\n \n🔔 Follow me for more unfiltered leadership stories and self-coaching insights for leaders building with purpose.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-your-biggest-leadership-strength-activity-7349063235876073473-hdv7?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

      {
        "urn": "7348683525962801152",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7348683524868063233",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-09 14:04:44",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1752062684527
        },
        "text": "🚨 CEOs & founders - are you actively scaling yourself, not just your company?\n\nLeadership isn’t static. And in a world of constant change, the most successful leaders aren't just reactive - they're self-coaching ahead of the curve.\n\nHere are 5 high-impact strategies I share with execs ready to transform how they lead:\n\n✅ Adapt Your Leadership Style\nEmpathy + agility = essentials for managing a multi-generational team.\n\n✅ Embrace Tech & Innovation\nLeadership must evolve alongside your tech stack. Coaching should too.\n\n✅ Reflect on Long-Term Goals\nVision isn’t a one-off strategy - it’s a daily alignment practice.\n\n✅ Commit to Continuous Growth\nStaying still is falling behind. Invest in your evolution.\n\n✅ Lead Through Coaching\n\nYour team’s ceiling is your growth floor. Model what you expect.\n\n📄 I’ve captured these strategies in a simple visual guide.\n \n👉 Swipe through the PDF to see how leaders are applying them in real time. \n-----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n🔔 Follow me for more interviews, insights, and self-coaching tools for leaders and founders building with purpose.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_5-high-impact-leadership-strategies-activity-7348683525962801152-fDj3?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

        "document": {
          "title": "5 high-impact leadership strategies",
          "page_count": 5,
          "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/document/media/v2/D4E1FAQGnBHGDt827PA/feedshare-document-sanitized-pdf/B4EZfvEZjeHsA8-/0/1752062663731?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=2NJIzzTCMI714gijIAoeVKRWTWTUyTSNLQo8ZaduOuM",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E1FAQGnBHGDt827PA/feedshare-document-cover-images_1920/B4EZfvEZjeHsAw-/0/1752062664733?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=LUN9sXPAdrAMZSFu-NJFMV3-QJ7qXgplLnBsl8YoO20"
        }

      {
        "urn": "7348308632645591041",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7348305760172343297",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-08 13:15:02",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1751973302995
        },
        "text": "What if the key to leadership wasn’t confidence - but belonging?\n\nWhen Sarah Walker-Smith walked into the final interview for her CEO role, she tore up the polished pitch. \n\nInstead of leading with credentials, she led with her story - as a girl raised on a council estate, navigating life without a safety net, and fighting to be seen.\n\nThat choice changed everything.\n\nNow the CEO of AMPA and Chair of Eden Project North, Sarah leads 1,500+ people across the UK - and she does it by being radically human.\n\nEssential lessons from Sarah’s journey:\n\n➡️ Belonging > Pretending: Fitting in isn’t the same as showing up as yourself\n\n➡️ Lead Like a Creator: Storytelling, musical theatre, and bold ideas fuel her leadership style\n\n➡️ Power Has Purpose: She uses her voice to amplify others still waiting to be heard\n\n➡️ Trust Starts with You: Knowing your own story helps you meet people where they are\n\n➡️ Resilience is Earned: Her past didn’t hold her back - it shaped how she leads with empathy today\n\nSarah’s episode is a must for anyone leading from the margins - or rewriting the rules from the inside.\n\n👀 Watch the full episode: https://lnkd.in/eh7rHC37\n\n-------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n🎧 Subscribe to Confessions of a Successful Leader YouTube channel: https://lnkd.in/epA9ttWA\n\n🔔 Follow me for more interviews, insights, and self-coaching tools for leaders and founders building with purpose.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-the-key-to-leadership-wasnt-confidence-activity-7348308632645591041-pM6V?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

          "type": "video",
          "url": "https://dms.licdn.com/playlist/vid/v2/D4E05AQHRlnniaP6sFQ/mp4-720p-30fp-crf28/B4EZfptJ2pHgB0-/0/1751972674186?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=5pFKbPK_iDMoRiRrZBkrgePNDQaTwT88_o19qOICocQ",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E05AQHRlnniaP6sFQ/videocover-high/B4EZfptJ2pHgCM-/0/1751972668123?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=uqIuCKcp3k8KS5qUrd6-tqOvdCo7G2TUXDKLWEgSdyE"
        }

      {
        "urn": "7348263760924434433",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7348263760924434433",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-08 10:16:44",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1751962604743
        },
        "text": "They turned up late. Again.\nAnd I felt... disrespected.\n\nBut then I paused.\nAnd got honest with myself.\n\nIt wasn’t just about being late.\nIt was about what it triggered in me.\n\n👉 “I’m not valued.”\n👉 “My time doesn’t matter.”\n👉 “They wouldn’t do this to others.”\n\nI could’ve called them out.\nInstead, I called myself in.\n\nI asked:\nWhere am I still seeking approval?\nWhat story am I carrying from the past?\nWhat part of me needs attention, not blame?\n\nThis is the inner work.\nNot easy. But essential.\n\nBecause self-leadership isn’t about control.\nIt’s about compassion.\n\nEspecially for the parts of us\nthat still need to feel seen.\n\n🪞When you feel triggered, start within.\n\nP.S. What helps others shift\nfrom reaction to reflection?\n\n♻️ Repost to help others do the inner work too.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_they-turned-up-late-again-and-i-felt-activity-7348263760924434433-0yHa?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

      {
        "urn": "7348255330314645504",

        "text": "I am grateful that my client work invites me to travel around the world. I have returned to the channel islands this week.       {
        "urn": "7346877975818108928",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7346875038295289857",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-04 14:30:07",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1751632207827
        },
        "text": "The need to be right is the fastest way to block your own growth.\n\nThis week’s newsletter features Marshall Goldsmith - legendary coach to Fortune 500 CEOs - on what really holds leaders back: ego disguised as ambition.\n\nInside:\n\n🪞 How to spot when you’re leading to impress - not to impact\n\n🧠 3 self-reflection habits to keep your ego in check\n\n🏁 Why legacy isn’t what you build - it’s what you leave behind\n\nIf you're scaling a team or shaping a culture, this is your reminder: your biggest leadership breakthrough might start with letting go.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_the-need-to-be-right-is-the-fastest-way-to-activity-7346877975818108928-AMao?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 8,
          "like": 7,
          "support": 0,
          "love": 0,
          "insight": 0,
          "celebrate": 1,
          "funny": 0,
          "comments": 5,
          "reposts": 0
        },
        "article": {
          "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/pulse/beyond-spotlight-leading-what-matters-andrew-tallents-ttute?trackingId=b5HZ56ggHNtHAJE%2BlW7N3w%3D%3D",
          "title": "Beyond the Spotlight: Leading for What Matters",
          "subtitle": "Andrew Tallents",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E12AQHRYND0d_xYAA/article-cover_image-shrink_720_1280/B4EZfR1iciGcAI-/0/1751572176530?e=1761177600&v=beta&t=ShQu_sdjfBd7ICN7bSW_kKWhOEK3aj79k4X198QcTas"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7346500490987233282",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7346476145992761344",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-03 13:30:08",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1751542208430
        },
        "text": "What do you see when you look out the window… \nthat others still don’t notice?\n\nThat question changed Sarah McArthur’s life - and helped define her purpose.\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, I spoke with Sarah McArthur, editor, author, and mentee of Frances Hesselbein, about how a childhood spent traveling the world in a VW van shaped the way she sees leadership, legacy, and what it means to serve.\n\nSarah’s story is quiet, reflective - and deeply powerful.\n\nLessons from Sarah’s journey:\n\n➡️ Observation is a superpower - true leadership starts with paying attention.\n\n➡️ Legacy is in the lives we touch, not just the titles we hold.\n\n➡️ Proximity builds empathy - living in motion taught her to see humanity up close.\n\n➡️ Purpose is persistent - it follows you, even if you don’t name it at first.\n\n➡️ Leadership is inclusion - everyone deserves a voice in the outcome.\n\nSarah reminds us that leadership isn’t just about what we say - it’s about what we notice, what we nurture, and how we show up when no one’s watching.\n\n👀 Watch the full episode: https://lnkd.in/ebwvcg9H\n------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n🎧 Subscribe to Confessions of a Successful Leader YouTube channel: https://lnkd.in/epA9ttWA\n\n🔔 Follow me for more interviews, insights, and self-coaching tools for leaders and founders building with purpose.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-do-you-see-when-you-look-out-the-window-activity-7346500490987233282-T0A4?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {

        "media": {
          "type": "video",
          "url": "https://dms.licdn.com/playlist/vid/v2/D4E05AQFK0gvd8ZsB7A/mp4-720p-30fp-crf28/B4EZfPtISyHsBw-/0/1751536491416?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=IuCXHu4HCe95SbXy8CPrMexhJBMP9P3QvUlTmcmJaHY",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E05AQFK0gvd8ZsB7A/videocover-high/B4EZfPtISyHsCI-/0/1751536485508?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=BWxJfX1WkWNYKCaXRS05Y4HqYMYNhZCGI7t3ujilV2U"
        }

      {
        "urn": "7346144429457502208",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7346144428253757440",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-02 13:55:16",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1751457316746
        },
        "text": "Leaders are moving. Are you paying attention to the shifts?\n\nFrom unexpected CEO exits to strategic hires at global powerhouses - this week offers three sharp reminders:\n\n📌 Leadership changes are more than headlines. They're signals of deeper transitions inside companies.\n\n📌 Strategy isn’t static. It lives and breathes through the people at the top.\n\n1️⃣ Domino’s Australian CEO resigns after just 1 year in the role - highlighting how misalignment at the top can create ripple effects fast. \n(source: https://lnkd.in/eSbVkVWa)\n\n2️⃣ UBS doubles down on tech M&A, hiring a Morgan Stanley veteran to lead its U.S. expansion. A bold move that speaks volumes about where the bank sees growth. \n(source: https://lnkd.in/emRgJkEf)\n\n3️⃣ Boeing appoints a long-time insider to steer its Defense division - stability through trusted leadership at a time of heightened scrutiny. \n(source: https://lnkd.in/eudYp97r)\n\nEvery executive transition is a reflection point.\n\nAs a CEO or founder - ask yourself:\n→ Where is your leadership aligned?\n→ Where is it resisting change?\n\nFollow me for weekly clarity like this - and if this was helpful, repost to share the signal with your network.\n\n----------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#LeadershipCompass #ExecutiveLeadership #CEOInsights #BoardroomMoves #FounderWisdom #BusinessStrategy #LeadershipTransitions #SelfCoaching",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_leadership-compass-w26-activity-7346144429457502208-MxbD?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {

        "document": {
          "title": "Leadership Compass - W26",
          "page_count": 6,
          "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/document/media/v2/D4E1FAQG9xCxY9-QMwA/feedshare-document-chunked-pdf/B4EZfK.TYMGcBo-/0/1751457240334?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=HLMHLkXH4tFUdUwmA8hhhsz6C4bIedzBI5fs-VdoeSE",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E1FAQG9xCxY9-QMwA/feedshare-document-cover-images_1920/B4EZfK.TYMGcAw-/0/1751457246636?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=OsVOJlqw6pqmCsR_U7XGhequxFeSMz6V-PIpVtHZeAw"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7345783266299273216",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7345777728010305536",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-07-01 14:00:08",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1751371208739
        },
        "text": "What if your biggest career breakthrough came… \nfrom being called out for trying too hard to impress?\n\nThat’s exactly what happened to Marshall Goldsmith, one of the world’s most celebrated leadership thinkers and bestselling authors.\n\nOn Confessions of a Successful Leader, Marshall shares how a moment of brutal honesty in a PhD seminar shaped the coaching philosophy that’s impacted millions.\n\nEssential lessons from Marshall’s journey:\n\n➡️ Success doesn't mean significance\n\n➡️ If you're not getting better, you're standing still\n\n➡️ Great coaches don’t give advice - they hold up mirrors\n\n➡️ You can’t lead others until you lead yourself\n\n➡️ Legacy isn’t what you take - it’s what you give away\n\nFrom 11 million frequent flyer miles to founding the 100 Coaches movement, Marshall’s story proves that leadership is less about control - and more about contribution.\n\n🎧 Listen to the episode: https://lnkd.in/epHRXjNW\n\n🔔 Follow me for more unfiltered leadership stories, self-coaching tools, and lessons from the world’s top performers.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-your-biggest-career-breakthrough-activity-7345783266299273216-haUN?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 5,
          "like": 4,
          "support": 0,
          "love": 0,

        "media": {
          "type": "video",
          "url": "https://dms.licdn.com/playlist/vid/v2/D4E05AQHiuhygoKaSTg/mp4-720p-30fp-crf28/B4EZfFx7AoHgCA-/0/1751369958041?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=kbIr81yd2rTv8ZKUyVhMq117JKTFoKvEvo3eolQHY74",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E05AQHiuhygoKaSTg/videocover-high/B4EZfFx7AoHgCY-/0/1751369953157?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=LiIRhSoGYoDdrdI6M1xn0bQ23J749ReVA0ZOnrJKpQ0"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7344329916580061185",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7344325884360749057",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-27 13:45:03",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1751024703164
        },
        "text": "You don’t need a plane crash to wake up to what matters.\n\nThis week’s edition features Dave Sanderson, survivor of the “Miracle on the Hudson,” who shares what crisis really reveals about leadership - and why self-leadership is the foundation of everything.\n\nInside:\n🧠 How to lead with clarity when chaos hits\n🔥 A crisis checklist every CEO should know\n🧭 Why the most certain person - not the smartest - becomes the leader\n\nThis is for founders ready to stop reacting and start leading from within 👇",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_you-dont-need-a-plane-crash-to-wake-up-to-activity-7344329916580061185-C-jW?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 9,
          "like": 7,
          "support": 0,
          "love": 0,
          "insight": 0,

          "funny": 0,

        },
        "article": {
          "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/pulse/crisis-clarity-when-leadership-gets-real-andrew-tallents-vfaje?trackingId=zsCpplvbPBTjk2SLM6th9A%3D%3D",
          "title": "Crisis and Clarity: When Leadership Gets Real",
          "subtitle": "Andrew Tallents",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E12AQGq6OyKPIhLVw/article-cover_image-shrink_720_1280/B4EZexHXIvGwAI-/0/1751023192088?e=1761177600&v=beta&t=KRygMrhhIFwrVGFBFOi-3bz15y5qP5ARM-DThsAftz8"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7343971389961371648",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7343971297850273792",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-26 14:00:23",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750939223757
        },
        "text": "What if the thing you were judged for at 16… \n\nbecame your superpower at 30?\n\nWhen Lauren Tickner started posting about fitness online as a teenager, she was mocked, dismissed, even bullied.\n\nNow she’s a global entrepreneur, speaker, and founder helping thousands of people build freedom-first businesses - and she’s doing it her way.\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, Lauren shares a raw, real story of resilience, reinvention, and rethinking what leadership truly means.\n\n💡 Key lessons from Lauren’s journey:\n\n➡️ Judgment fades - your values stay\n\n➡️ Freedom is built, not found\n\n➡️ Leadership is learned, not inherited\n\n➡️ Remote trust starts with clear systems, not control\n\n➡️ Your biggest leap often begins with discomfort\n\nLauren’s episode is a must-listen for anyone breaking the rules to build what’s never been done before.\n\n👀 Watch the full episode: https://lnkd.in/ey-Jvg9v\n---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n🎧 Subscribe to Confessions of a Successful Leader YouTube channel: https://lnkd.in/epA9ttWA\n\n🔔 Follow me for more interviews, insights, and self-coaching tools for leaders and founders building with purpose.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-the-thing-you-were-judged-for-at-activity-7343971389961371648-FlRX?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {

      {
        "urn": "7343909151481233409",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7343909151481233409",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-26 09:53:04",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750924384947
        },
        "text": "Well what a week for our podcast. \n\nLast Thursday Simon Squibb was our guest, yesterday Dave Sanderson shared his story about The Miracle on the Hudson and today we are releasing the episode where Lauren Tickner shares her energy for a life worth living (she was in Bali when it was recorded! and I was in Liverpool 😂. Next week we are honoured to have the great grandfather of coaching Marshall Goldsmith on the show.\n\nWatch out for the full episode with Lauren Tickner later today. If you are a Founder or CEO who wants to share your story about trauma informed leadership then DM me the word \"TRAUMA\" and we can have a chat.\n\nhttps://lnkd.in/evC2ztmE",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_confessions-of-a-successful-leader-activity-7343909151481233409-u7cD?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {

      {
        "urn": "7343622027972341760",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7343622027972341760",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-25 14:52:09",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750855929368
        },
        "text": "Most people don’t need new strategies.\n They need a new story about what’s possible.\n\nWe don’t get stuck because of skill.\n We get stuck because of belief.\n\nLast week, I spoke with Sandy on TALRadio English.\n We unpacked the real reason leaders stay small:\n 👉 Self-sabotage disguised as logic\n 👉 Growth-limiting beliefs inherited years ago\n 👉 The quiet fear of “what if I fail?”\n\nIf any of that rings true for you,\n this episode might be exactly what you need today.\n 🎧 Shift your mindset.\n 🔥 Step into your potential.\n 🛠️ Use self-coaching tools that actually work.\n\nHere’s the conversation:\n 🔗 https://lnkd.in/e-CetEem\n\nP.S. What’s one belief others have outgrown, but still holds them back?\n♻️ Repost if this might help others move forward.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_overcoming-self-limiting-beliefs-identifying-activity-7343622027972341760-utrl?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 8,
          "like": 8,
          "support": 0,

          "insight": 0,

        "media": {
          "type": "image",

        "article": {
          "url": "https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=44HTpFQtJMM",
          "title": "Overcoming Self-Limiting Beliefs Identifying and Defeating Internal Barriers | Empower Yourself - 20",
          "subtitle": "youtube.com",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/sync/v2/D4E27AQF7uQSr99hhGQ/articleshare-shrink_1280_800/B4EZenJL1JHcAU-/0/1750855893105?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=QVhG95ns0euCSQzOSTCr8xjC_QfgGwfWVAejelocJkg"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7343618598017396737",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7343618596683608067",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-25 14:38:31",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750855111603
        },
        "text": "Leaders are stepping into new territory - are you ready to navigate yours?\n\nThis week’s Leadership Compass brings you 3 powerful stories shaping how leadership is evolving right now:\n\n🔹 MI6 appoints its first-ever female Chief - Blaise Metreweli - marking a bold shift in intelligence leadership.\nSource: https://lnkd.in/ehi5iSea\n\n🔹 Nestlé proposes Pablo Isla (ex-Inditex CEO) as its next Chair, signaling a break from internal succession and a return to fundamentals.\nSource: https://lnkd.in/eFYKNG3K\n\n🔹 India Inc is no longer hiring for comfort - they're hunting leaders who thrive in pressure, lead through chaos, and steady the storm.\nSource: https://lnkd.in/ecm8mp55\n\nHere’s what it all tells me:\n\nLeadership isn’t about being the smartest in the room anymore.\n\nIt’s about resilience, adaptability, and the courage to steer through uncertainty - publicly and powerfully.\n\n👉 Which of these stories speaks most to where you are in your leadership journey?\n\n-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n🔁 Repost to keep your network informed.\n👤 Follow me for weekly executive insights.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_leadership-compass-activity-7343618598017396737-Jjvc?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

          "page_count": 6,
          "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/document/media/v2/D4E1FAQG-hnTzAMdERw/feedshare-document-chunked-pdf/B4EZenFthGGwBo-/0/1750855046096?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=lb8RFi6j886BROtYXaMcHc8ImHP4jpkkYUdgb6TJvcc",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E1FAQG-hnTzAMdERw/feedshare-document-cover-images_1920/B4EZenFthGGwAw-/0/1750855049566?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=jz7qZQBUU_KVhGZxr6AvLj27vLZGwu-ngfO86Op_WgY"
        }

      {
        "urn": "7343572731835113472",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7343572731835113472",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-25 11:36:16",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750844176253
        },
        "text": "A year ago I hosted Dave Conway on my podcast. I had just met Dave in Canada at one of his entrepreneur retreats. It changed my life. \n\nDave said to me \" so you want to enable millions of leaders around the world to live their professional and personal dreams?, How much desire do you have to do that Andrew? How much do you want to be the one that makes that a reality?\" \n\nSince that day I have worked every day to build my business around helping any leader around the world to learn how to lead and coach themselves more effectively. Thanks Dave. You can watch the whole podcast here https://lnkd.in/e8QFzPeU",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_012-dave-conway-short-activity-7343572731835113472-2gm0?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",

      {
        "urn": "7343512019809038336",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7339368806219231232",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-25 07:35:01",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750829701378
        },
        "text": "I am excited to let you know that our second Walkabout Wirral, the newest group formed from the original Walkabout London, founded by Ben Brabyn is on the move again on the 26th June.\n\nWalkabout Wirral brings together leaders of all ages who want to think more clearly, connect more deeply, and act more decisively—simply by walking together. It’s about fresh air, open minds, and powerful conversations.\n\nMeeting on the 4th Thursday of every month, our second Walkabout Wirral is at 5pm on June 26th in West Kirby. If you work in Merseyside or are visiting the Wirral that day and would like to come along for a one hour walk around the lake and along the Wirral Way then please sign up here\n\nhttps://lnkd.in/eYWd-39T\n\nFor those who want to join us later we will be quenching our thirsts in the West Kirby Tap from 6pm onwards.\n\nI know that great things will happen as a result of this group walking together every month. Are you ready?",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_i-am-excited-to-let-you-know-that-our-second-activity-7339368806219231232-6gid?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "repost",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

      {
        "urn": "7343504718041608195",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7342174586903445505",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-25 07:06:00",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750827960501
        },
        "text": "Which question would you start with?\n If you had an AI Coach in your pocket today…\n Which of these 3 questions would you explore first?\n\n🧭 \"What’s really getting in the way of me making progress right now?\"\n🧠 \"How can I lead myself better in the moments that matter?\"\n🔥 \"What one change would have the biggest impact on my leadership?\"\n\nThese are real questions CEOs and Founders like you ask every day.\n\n And now, they’re asking them to their AI Coach—\n Right in the flow of work.\n \nI’ll post a real AI Coach demo answering the most popular one.\n\nWant to try it for yourself?\n\n DM me the word COACH and I’ll send you free access.\n#leadership #selfcoaching #founders #ceo #aicoach #selfleadership",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_leadership-selfcoaching-founders-ugcPost-7342174586903445505-ZRuH?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "repost",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

      {
        "urn": "7343265718890639361",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7343265718164942848",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-24 15:16:18",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750770978663
        },
        "text": "Lovely to see Kevin Chapman making me giggle again",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_leadership-stress-coaching-activity-7343265718890639361-yipz?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "quote",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 4,
          "like": 3,
          "support": 0,
          "love": 1,
          "insight": 0,
          "celebrate": 0,
          "funny": 0,
          "comments": 2,
          "reposts": 0
        },
        "reshared_post": {
          "urn": "7343257022575833090",
          "posted_at": {
            "date": "2025-06-24 14:41:45",
            "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
            "timestamp": 1750768905300
          },
          "text": "I was so confident when I qualified as a coach until this happened (and in my first ever paid coaching session!!!)\n\nHave you ever had an eye-watering experience like this?\n\nA wonderful conversation with the aptly named Andrew Tallents earlier this year as part of his superb Confessions of a Successful Leader series. \n\nA gentle conversation about my views and experience as a leader... ventured into comedy, forgiveness, smoky underpants, murderous doctors, piano teachers and why my children are anxious (and are schools to blame).\n\nWe dive a little into the wonders of state management and embodiment and recount a few of my life's memories and insights into leadership!\n\nMy greatest success .... was remembering them!\n\nCheck out the our full conversation here:\n https://lnkd.in/eacp8s9B\n\nand enjoy the murderous story below!\n\nAnd Andrew's full series here:\nhttps://lnkd.in/e7B7i2jw\n\n#leadership\n#stress\n#coaching",
          "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/kevinchapman-rose_leadership-stress-coaching-activity-7343257022575833090-vg5y?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
          "post_type": "regular",
          "author": {
            "first_name": "Kevin",
            "last_name": "Chapman",
            "headline": "Leadership and Wellbeing | Communication and Impact | Coach, Consultant, Trainer",
            "username": "kevinchapman-rose",
            "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/kevinchapman-rose?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAFMGzUByQ7m8xcK4yg1OJzB7Vy-LRu0qTY",
            "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E03AQFJrc0q_cZ_iQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/B4EZeN6IfiHcAg-/0/1750432517264?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=VwZqRaFn4AmRkx1J59mEnJal_zRIOM8a52RGcF1K7Lc"

      {
        "urn": "7343246742131236864",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7343246741107798017",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-24 14:00:54",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750766454251
        },
        "text": "What if your defining leadership moment came not in a boardroom - but in a sinking plane?\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, I sat down with @Dave Sanderson, author, speaker, and the last passenger off Flight 1549 - better known as the Miracle on the Hudson.\n\nDave’s story isn’t just about surviving impact.\n\nIt’s about leading when it matters most.\n\n💡 Lessons from Dave’s remarkable journey:\n\n➡️ Certainty Creates Leadership: People follow the calmest voice in chaos.\n\n➡️ Personal Leadership Comes First: If you can’t lead yourself, no one will follow.\n\n➡️ Live Your Values, Always: Dave chose to help others before saving himself - because he’d prepared for that moment his whole life.\n\n➡️ Proximity is Power: Sitting at the right table changes how you lead.\n\n➡️ Legacy is Wisdom Shared: His mission now? To pass every hard-earned lesson on.\n\nThis episode is a must for anyone who wants to lead with courage, clarity, and character - no matter what life throws at you.\n\n👀 Watch the full episode: https://lnkd.in/eKH5pRaz\n\n---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n🎧 Subscribe to Confessions of a Successful Leader YouTube channel: https://lnkd.in/epA9ttWA\n\n🔔 Follow me for more interviews, insights, and self-coaching tools for leaders and founders building with purpose.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-your-defining-leadership-moment-came-activity-7343246742131236864-VDDc?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

      {
        "urn": "7342887061827317760",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7342887061827317760",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-23 14:11:39",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750680699784
        },
        "text": "73% of high-performing managers never make it to executive level.\n\nThe reason? It’s not talent. It’s mindset.\n\nMost managers stay stuck because they’re playing yesterday’s game - focused on execution, not transformation.\n\nHere’s the truth:\n\n💡 Directors aren’t discovered - they’re championed.\nYou need visibility beyond your team. Senior leaders won’t see your impact if you stay siloed.\n\n💡 Excellence has an expiration date.\n At the executive level, it’s no longer about “Can you execute?” but “Can you transform?” Your value shifts from personal output to building organizational capability.\n\n💡 You can’t delegate your way to strategic impact.\nThe shift from “I solve problems” to “I develop problem-solvers” is key. It’s your ticket to leadership at scale.\n\n💡 Your network is your net worth.\nManagers with cross-functional networks advance 2.4x faster. Invest in relationships outside your immediate team.\n\n💡 Ambiguity is a career killer.\nYou must be able to articulate your leadership value clearly. Clarity is key to getting noticed by senior leaders.\n\nThe breakthrough:\n📍 Start thinking like a CEO now - solve problems no one asked you to fix.\n📍 Take ownership of organizational challenges, not just wins.\n📍 Build strategic visibility beyond your department.\n📍 Treat your role as executive training, not a finish line.\n\nReady to elevate your leadership?\n\n---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n🔔 Follow me for more interviews, insights, and self-coaching tools for leaders and founders building with purpose.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_73-of-high-performing-managers-never-make-activity-7342887061827317760-Tsyu?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",

      {
        "urn": "7342174588123947008",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7342174586903445505",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-21 15:00:32",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750510832816
        },
        "text": "Which question would you start with?\n If you had an AI Coach in your pocket today…\n Which of these 3 questions would you explore first?\n\n🧭 \"What’s really getting in the way of me making progress right now?\"\n🧠 \"How can I lead myself better in the moments that matter?\"\n🔥 \"What one change would have the biggest impact on my leadership?\"\n\nThese are real questions CEOs and Founders like you ask every day.\n\n And now, they’re asking them to their AI Coach—\n Right in the flow of work.\n \nI’ll post a real AI Coach demo answering the most popular one.\n\nWant to try it for yourself?\n\n DM me the word COACH and I’ll send you free access.\n#leadership #selfcoaching #founders #ceo #aicoach #selfleadership",

      {
        "urn": "7341797754643378177",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7341797754010001413",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-20 14:03:08",
          "relative": "1 month ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750420988713
        },
        "text": "Today’s talent isn’t lazy. They’re just done working without purpose.\n\nThis week’s newsletter features entrepreneur and investor Simon Squibb, whose journey from homeless teen to global founder flips our definition of success on its head.\n\nInside:\n\n 🔹 What the next generation really wants at work\n\n 🔹 How to lead with purpose (without losing performance)\n\n 🔹 Why giving ownership early builds loyalty faster than perks ever could\n\nIf you’re building a business that’s meant to last, this one’s for you.\nRead the full edition 👇",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_todays-talent-isnt-lazy-theyre-just-done-activity-7341797754643378177-IKTy?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {

          "timestamp": 1750359537267
        },
        "text": "🚨 It’s live.\n\nOur AI Coach for leaders is now available — and we’re inviting leaders like you to try it for free.\n\nThis is the same framework we use inside our world class coaching systems… now available 24/7 via AI.\n\nIt’s built to help you self-coach, think clearly, and lead with intention.\n\nBut we’re still deciding how to shape the experience moving forward…\n\nWe’d love your take 👇\n\nIf you were to take up a free trial of this kind of AI Coach, which would you prefer?\n\nA) Unlimited time access, but limited messages\nB) Unlimited messages, but only available for a limited time\n\nDrop A or B in the comments.\n\nAnd if you want to give the AI Coach a go — DM me the word COACH and I’ll send you instant access.\n\nP.S. Feel free to repost this — especially if you know other leaders who love trying new tools ♻️",

        "author": {

          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

      {
        "urn": "7341465606191263745",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7341465606191263745",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-19 16:03:18",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Edited • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750341798351
        },
        "text": "The ownership gap isn't just a mindset issue — it's your self-coaching moment.\n\nThis week, I read a powerful piece by Justin Welsh on “The Ownership Gap.”\nYou can read it here: [https://lnkd.in/dKSb64sz)\n\nIn it, Justin shares something I’ve witnessed time and again as a leadership coach:\n\n“While the victims are explaining why the problem isn’t their fault, the owners are figuring out how to solve it.”\n\nThat line hit home.\n\nBecause the greatest shift I’ve seen in high-performing leaders isn’t strategic.\nIt’s \"internal\".\n\nIt’s when they stop waiting for someone else to change the rules — and start learning to play the game better.\n\nIt’s not about blame.\nIt’s not about perfection.\nIt’s about \"agency.\"\n\nThis is what self-coaching is all about.\n\nIt’s that quiet voice inside that says:\n\n“What part did I play?”\n“What can I learn here?”\n“What’s one thing I can do differently?”\n\nToo many of us — even CEOs and Founders — get stuck outsourcing our power.\n\nTo the market.\nTo our team.\nTo our upbringing.\nTo timing, luck, or ‘the system’.\n\nAnd when we do that, we widen the gap.\n\nBecause as Justin says:\n\n“One approach creates momentum. The other creates excuses.”\n\nSelf-coaching is how we choose momentum.\n\nSo here’s my invitation to you:\n\nWhat’s one area in your life — professional or personal — where you’ve been pointing fingers instead of stepping forward?\n\nTake a breath.\nTake ownership.\nTake one bold step.\n\nBecause the gap only closes when \"you\" cross it.\n\n🟢 If you’re ready to take that step and want support, I’ve created a free Self-Coaching Starter Pack.\n\nJust comment “I’m in” and I’ll send it over.\n\nLet’s start building a life you love — one decision at a time.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_the-ownership-gap-isnt-just-a-mindset-issue-activity-7341465606191263745-6v55?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

          "type": "image",

              "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4D10AQHN-Fj5YImyyA/image-shrink_1280/B4DZeIgDtBHkAM-/0/1750341796920?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=HzyCBDdob0r-E4F32ptpBGf39ByFqBs7sA2rbTMYFu8",

      },
      {
        "urn": "7341419499725684736",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7341416697758715907",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-19 13:00:05",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750330805713
        },
        "text": "What if the worst thing that ever happened to you… \n\nwas also the moment your life truly began?\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, I sat down with Simon Squibb - entrepreneur, investor, and founder of HelpBnk - whose story is anything but ordinary.\n\nHomeless at 15.\n\nRetired by 40.\n\nNow on a mission to help 1 million people start a business.\n\n💡 Lessons from Simon’s extraordinary journey:\n\n➡️Adversity Wakes You Up: Being left alone at 8 - and homeless at 15 - sparked the self-reliance that built a global business career.\n\n➡️Purpose > Paycheck: Money brought freedom. Purpose brought meaning.\n\n➡️Entrepreneurship is a Muscle: And everyone has it. You just need to train it.\n\n➡️Redefine Retirement: Simon doesn’t believe in switching off - he believes in being on purpose.\n\n➡️Own Your Time: His definition of success? Total freedom to make an impact.\n\nThis episode is a masterclass in reframing failure, rethinking success, and rebuilding life on your own terms.\n\n👀 Watch the full episode: https://lnkd.in/drvkP5Kn\n\n🎧 Subscribe to Confessions of a Successful Leader YouTube channel: https://lnkd.in/epA9ttWA\n---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n🔔 Follow me for more interviews, insights, and self-coaching tools for leaders and founders building with purpose.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_what-if-the-worst-thing-that-ever-happened-activity-7341419499725684736-sd6F?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",

      {

          "timestamp": 1750268118424
        },
        "text": "Do you keep getting in your own way when trying to achieve your goals?\n\nToday I'm giving away my unique free guide on how to get out of your own way so you can be more successful in life.\n\nIt's a blueprint to live a worry free life. \n\nWant access 100% free?\n\nReply SUCCESS and I'll DM the link",

      {
        "urn": "7341073701523603456",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7341073700424704001",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-18 14:06:00",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750248360997
        },
        "text": "Leadership isn’t static - it’s rotational, surprising, and sometimes overdue.\n\nHere’s what surfaced this week in The Leadership Compass:\n🍗 Yum! Brands promotes from within\nChris Turner, CFO since 2019, is stepping up as CEO.\nFinance leaders are no longer just behind the numbers-they’re leading the vision.\n\n👜 Renault’s CEO is heading to Gucci\nYes, you read that right.\nLuca de Meo moves from automotive to fashion, becoming CEO of Kering.\nIt’s a bold reminder: industry lines don’t define leadership.\n\n📉 CEO turnover just hit a record high\nNearly 15% of S&P 500 firms lost their CEOs in Q1.\nAlmost half of replacements were external.\nThe reason? Shaky succession plans and thin mid-level benches.\n\n💭 This week’s questions:\nWho’s being built to step in - not just step up?\nAnd are you hiring for today, or grooming for tomorrow?\n\n🔁 Repost if this hit close - and follow me for The Leadership Compass every week.",

First 50 signed up get a VIP onboarding with me and the creators.\n\n#SelfLeadership #AIForLeaders #CoachingInFlow #SelfCoaching #Founders #CEOs",

      {
        "urn": "7340417430554353665",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7340417430554353665",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-16 18:38:13",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750091893805
        },
        "text": "I’ve coached 100s of teams — my biggest mistakes?\n\n(At first, I thought every team could be coached)\n\nBut the truth is that a team needs to feel safe first\n\nAvoid my mistakes and:\n\n1. Make sure you take your team offsite\n2. Engage an external facilitator to coach the team\n3. Spend the first part of the day creating safety to speak\n\nThe result? A team that is more receptive to change!\n\nDon't just go straight in to doing. Create the space for being!\n\nWhat’s your best tip to get the most out of team coaching?\n\n♻️ P.S. Repost to help other CEOs & Founders",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_ive-coached-100s-of-teams-my-biggest-mistakes-activity-7340417430554353665-h0ZA?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {

      {
        "urn": "7340362247627591681",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7340362247627591681",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-16 14:58:57",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1750078737170
        },
        "text": "Are you focusing on the right 20% of your actions?\n\nAs CEOs and Founders, we often fill our days with \"busy work,\" but the truth is, busy doesn't equal productive. \n\nTo maximize your leadership impact, it’s time to shift your focus to the 20% that truly moves the needle.\n\nHere’s how to apply the 80/20 rule to boost your productivity:\n\n1. Priorities\n➡️ Identify the 3 most important goals each day.\n➡️ Eliminate distractions by focusing only on these.\n➡️ Delegate everything else that doesn’t move the needle.\n\n2. Time Management\n➡️ Block off 1-2 hours of deep, uninterrupted focus.\n➡️ Protect this time like your business depends on it.\n➡️ No emails. No meetings. Just deep work.\n\n3. Decision Making\n➡️ Make fewer decisions to conserve mental energy.\n➡️ Create systems for small, routine choices.\n➡️ Save your brainpower for the high-impact decisions.\n\n4. Goals\n➡️ Focus on 3 key goals for the year - nothing more.\n➡️ Say “no” to distractions and irrelevant opportunities.\n➡️ Laser-focus creates massive, sustainable results.\n\nStop doing more. Start doing what matters most.\n\nThe 80/20 rule isn’t about being lazy - it’s about being strategic and intentional. \n\nFocus on the 20% that will drive your success as a leader.\n\n------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n♻️ Repost to add value to your network. \n🔔 Follow Andrew Tallents for more on self-coaching & leadership.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_are-you-focusing-on-the-right-20-of-your-activity-7340362247627591681-aJ1Q?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {

          "last_name": "Tallents",

      {
        "urn": "7339981154013372416",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7339981153413525504",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-15 13:44:37",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749987877372
        },
        "text": "The time has come for each and everyone of us to reclaim the hopes and dreams we had as children. It will not be easy but we as human beings have the ability to choose the lives we want to lead. Create the vision of who you want to be in this world, set clear goals from where you are right now and then focus all your energy in moving towards them with purpose. We all have the power to create the world we want to see. Will you be joining us? https://lnkd.in/ebq4vCHS\n\n#selfleadership #selfcoaching #consciousleadership",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_charliechaplin-thegreatdictator-speech-activity-7339981154013372416-3Rkz?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",

          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

        "reshared_post": {
          "urn": "7117831528855789569",
          "posted_at": {
            "date": "2023-10-11 13:21:19",
            "relative": "1 year ago • Edited • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
            "timestamp": 1697023279394
          },
          "text": "To me, this is the greatest speech ever written.\n\nIt's so famous that you'll have inevitably seen it several times before, but Chaplin's words – and the passion with which he delivers them – are so, so relevant to the modern world.\n\nIt also perfectly encapsulates my own feelings about the way things are right now. Humanity really hasn't learned anything since the 1940s, it seems.\n\nA masterclass way ahead of its time.\n\n#CharlieChaplin #TheGreatDictator #Speech",
          "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/arcopywriter_charliechaplin-thegreatdictator-speech-activity-7117831528855789569-J_qj?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",

      {
        "urn": "7339570586794246144",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7339570586794246144",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-14 10:33:10",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Edited • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749889990519
        },
        "text": "Jacinda Ardern’s new memoir, *A Different Kind of Power*, offers a compelling case for compassionate leadership. In it, she reflects on her journey from a small-town upbringing to becoming New Zealand's Prime Minister, emphasizing that qualities like empathy and kindness are not weaknesses but strengths in leadership. \n\nArdern's experiences underscore the importance of self-awareness and staying true to one's values, even in the face of immense challenges.\n\nThis perspective aligns closely with the principles of self-coaching. By cultivating self-awareness and emotional intelligence, leaders can navigate complex situations with authenticity and resilience. Ardern's emphasis on leading with compassion serves as a reminder that effective leadership starts from within.\n\nReflect on your own leadership journey. Are you leading with empathy and self-awareness? Consider how embracing these qualities can enhance your effectiveness and connection with others.\n\n#journey #leadership #memoir",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_journey-leadership-memoir-activity-7339570586794246144-_bJv?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",

      {
        "urn": "7339368806219231232",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7339368806219231232",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-13 21:11:22",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749841882281
        },
        "text": "I am excited to let you know that our second Walkabout Wirral, the newest group formed from the original Walkabout London, founded by Ben Brabyn is on the move again on the 26th June.\n\nWalkabout Wirral brings together leaders of all ages who want to think more clearly, connect more deeply, and act more decisively—simply by walking together. It’s about fresh air, open minds, and powerful conversations.\n\nMeeting on the 4th Thursday of every month, our second Walkabout Wirral is at 5pm on June 26th in West Kirby. If you work in Merseyside or are visiting the Wirral that day and would like to come along for a one hour walk around the lake and along the Wirral Way then please sign up here\n\nhttps://lnkd.in/eYWd-39T\n\nFor those who want to join us later we will be quenching our thirsts in the West Kirby Tap from 6pm onwards.\n\nI know that great things will happen as a result of this group walking together every month. Are you ready?",

      {
        "urn": "7339271766537564203",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7339271765707091968",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-13 14:45:46",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749818746218
        },
        "text": "The problem isn’t failure. The problem is thinking you’re not allowed to fail.\n\nThis week’s newsletter is packed with hard-earned leadership lessons founders rarely talk about - but absolutely need.\n\n🧨 How Failing Smarter Builds Better Leaders: Alvin Foo shares how repeated failure shaped his leadership across multiple industries - and why learning to fail well may be the most important founder skill of all.\n\n⚠️ The Leadership Shift No One Teaches You: Why your need to “hold it all together” might be silently breaking your leadership - and how self-coaching creates the space you keep avoiding.\n\n💡 Leadership doesn’t break you because it’s hard. It breaks you when you stop asking better questions.\n\n💬 What’s one leadership belief you’ve had to unlearn?\n\n------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n♻️ Repost to add value to your network. \n🔔 Follow Andrew Tallents for more on self-coaching & leadership.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_the-problem-isnt-failure-the-problem-is-activity-7339271766537564203-4dTr?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 5,
          "like": 4,
          "support": 0,
          "love": 1,
          "insight": 0,
          "celebrate": 0,
          "funny": 0,
          "comments": 2,
          "reposts": 0
        },
        "article": {
          "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/pulse/leading-without-certainty-growing-curiosity-andrew-tallents-t6b7e?trackingId=kNTQAnBqervg3l8w7%2BOqRw%3D%3D",
          "title": "Leading Without Certainty, Growing With Curiosity",
          "subtitle": "Andrew Tallents",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E12AQEAmwT3tVEXpQ/article-cover_image-shrink_720_1280/B4EZdpQQP1HgAM-/0/1749817563601?e=1761177600&v=beta&t=YFXuJ4ltXEq9MMsVrXr7uUAfAPadiMFg53cmVSVjyT4"
        }

      {
        "urn": "7338900822493130752",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7338900822493130752",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-12 14:11:46",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749730306266
        },
        "text": "Most CEOs won't admit this:\n\nThey're world-class at delivering shareholder value.\n\nBut privately?\n\nThey're frustrated that their personal dreams keep getting deferred.\n\nI’ve seen this pattern for 20 years.\n\nOnly 5% of leaders are truly effective at leading themselves.\n\n💡 Here’s why most leaders stay stuck:\n1️⃣ They believe success means sacrificing personal dreams.\n2️⃣ They outsource self-accountability.\n3️⃣ They wait for external permission to change.\n\nThe shift happens when you embrace self-coaching:\n\nGet brutally honest: What am I sacrificing right now?\n\nMap your “Wheel of Life” (career, family, health, friends, purpose).\n\n🎯 Take one daily action that moves you back into the driver’s seat.\n\nSelf-leadership isn’t a luxury skill.\n\nIt’s the foundation of sustainable success.\n\n------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n♻️ Repost to add value to your network. \n🔔 Follow Andrew Tallents for more on self-coaching & leadership.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_most-ceos-wont-admit-this-theyre-world-class-activity-7338900822493130752-VDGY?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

          "type": "image",
          "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E22AQHjH2yT4oI5NQ/feedshare-shrink_2048_1536/B4EZdkDZo4G4Ao-/0/1749730305482?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=BW9sLx3zQlBbKbZ_fPqn3zWgsIBJat_5WOVaZc-rfUU",
