      {
        "urn": "7338822694924169218",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7338822693506441216",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-12 09:01:19",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749711679202
        },
        "text": "What if joy, curiosity, and national impact were key indicators of leadership success?\n\nIn our latest episode, Sharad Mathur—veteran executive in the Indian insurance industry—shares a refreshing view on what it truly means to lead.\n\nThis isn’t your typical corporate playbook.\n\n✅ Discover how seeking joy in every task unlocks productivity.\n✅ Learn how family values and corporate culture can co-exist in high-performance environments.\n✅ Explore conscious leadership that empowers individuals and uplifts entire nations.\n\nSharad’s journey from curious child to visionary executive is a timely reminder:\nLeadership isn’t just about scale—it’s about soul.\n\n🎧 Tune in to this thought-provoking episode of Confessions of a Successful Leader.\n💡 Leave inspired to lead with more empathy, curiosity, and purpose.\n\n🔗 Watch here: https://lnkd.in/eJ8hZV4E\n\n#Leadership #SelfCoaching #India #ConsciousLeadership #GrowthMindset #Podcast #AndrewTallents #SharadMathur",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_leadership-selfcoaching-india-activity-7338822694924169218-PgS8?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 12,
          "like": 9,
          "support": 2,
          "love": 1,
          "insight": 0,
          "celebrate": 0,
          "funny": 0,
          "comments": 7,
          "reposts": 0
        },
        "media": {
          "type": "video",
          "url": "https://dms.licdn.com/playlist/vid/v2/D4E10AQGyhpeXfMsM_g/mp4-720p-30fp-crf28/B4EZdi8R5BHYBE-/0/1749711671259?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=04A6JPBWo5_l3qTeb_BfbkL3Y4VMUyoxqOLAN7Vul3I"
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7338543533370273792",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7338543532489551874",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-11 14:32:01",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749645121901
        },
        "text": "If you’re leading a company in 2025, you’re managing three things at once: people, capital, and uncertainty.\n\nHere’s what stood out in this week’s Leadership Compass:\n\n🚗 New CEO at Stellantis\nAntonio Filosa steps up, replacing Carlos Tavares.\nKnown for his collaborative leadership and operational chops, Filosa takes over as Stellantis looks to reset after a tough 2024.\n\nThe leadership handoff is about more than succession - it’s about strategy.\n\n🧵 Fashion M&A goes defensive\nHigh rates and global uncertainty are changing deal strategy.\nDick’s acquires Foot Locker ($2.4B).\nKontoor Brands buys Helly Hansen ($900M).\n\nDeals today aren’t about growth at all costs - they’re about stability and fit.\n\n🤖 AI is reshaping org structures\nIt’s not just productivity tools.\nAI is starting to change how companies organize, build teams, and execute work.\n\nLess hierarchy. More fluid, project-based structures.\n\n\n-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n🔁 Repost if this sparked something - and follow me for The Leadership Compass every week.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_leadership-compass-w22-activity-7338543533370273792-RMlW?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

        "document": {
          "title": "Leadership Compass - W22",
          "page_count": 6,
          "url": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/document/media/v2/D4E1FAQGCG4DYZRPF5Q/feedshare-document-sanitized-pdf/B4EZde.OiXHgBA-/0/1749645086610?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=_seYEx91XwBC796AeTltEElaw5UKrblbuF6M2moUBfw",
          "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4E1FAQGCG4DYZRPF5Q/feedshare-document-cover-images_1920/B4EZde.OiXHgA0-/0/1749645090889?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=ucVQXeKxbQKTVivcu9bugGftoA1Ibq8NgVSZ60SIhDE"

      {
        "urn": "7338535525261197312",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7338535525261197312",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-11 14:00:12",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749643212619
        },
        "text": "From Self-Doubt to Self-Leadership — My Journey to Empowering Change\n\nIt’s a privilege to share my story in this feature with Humans of Fuzia:\n 🟣 Empowering Leadership: A Journey of Self-Coaching and Transformation\n\n 👉 https://lnkd.in/eHkSP9hf\n\nFor too long, I believed leadership meant having all the answers.\n But real impact begins when we ask better questions—of ourselves.\n\nIn this piece, I reflect on:\nMoving beyond burnout to build a purposeful practice\nCoaching hundreds of CEOs and Founders to lead from the inside out\nWhy self-coaching is not a luxury—but a leadership necessity\n\nAnd how trusting yourself is often the most radical decision a leader can make\n\nThis isn’t just my story—it’s a call to all leaders who want to thrive by doing the inner work.\n\n🟢 I hope it encourages someone to pause.\n🟢 To turn inward.\n🟢 To lead with intention, not just ambition.\n\nIf you’ve ever felt pulled in every direction and wondered, “What about me?”—this is for you.\n\nClick here to access a free trial of our AI Coach in your pocket\n\nhttps://lnkd.in/eZxRwcfw\n\n💬 I’d love to know: What’s one belief about leadership you’ve let go of to become a better version of yourself?\n\n#Leadership #SelfCoaching #ExecutiveCoaching #FounderLife #HumansOfFuzia #Transformation #InnerWork #SelfLeadership #CEOcoach #SelfTrust",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_leadership-selfcoaching-executivecoaching-activity-7338535525261197312-nGYO?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

      {
        "urn": "7338276270348099586",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7338276270348099586",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-10 20:50:01",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749581401431
        },
        "text": "I have coached 100s of leaders stuck in draining relationships.\n(At first, they try to fix others or withdraw.)\nBut growth comes when you choose to shift.\n\nBe the adult in the room—not the parent or child.\n\nWant better dynamics?\n• Stop trying to control others.\n• Drop the need to be “right.”\n• Respond with maturity, not reaction.\n\nThe smartest person in the room often isn’t the wisest.\n\nThe key? Grown-up energy changes everything.\n\n🧠 How do you show up when tension rises?\n\n♻️ P.S. Repost to help others choose maturity over control.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_i-have-coached-100s-of-leaders-stuck-in-draining-activity-7338276270348099586-fFgY?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",

      {

        },
        "text": "James Richardson thanks for hosting me in this engaging conversation.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_new-podcast-with-andrew-tallents-founder-activity-7338170566039773185-iGdv?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "quote",
        "author": {

          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 7,
          "like": 5,

        "reshared_post": {
          "urn": "7338108935574335488",
          "posted_at": {
            "date": "2025-06-10 09:45:05",
            "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
            "timestamp": 1749541505712
          },
          "text": "🎙️ NEW PODCAST with Andrew Tallents – Founder of The Tallents Partnership\n\nWhat if the biggest thing holding your business back… is you?\n\nAndrew built a tech-enabled coaching model rooted in one powerful principle: founders must learn to lead themselves before they can lead others.\n\nIn this deep, honest conversation, we talk about:\n\n- How childhood beliefs create blindspots in founder behaviour\n- What triggers founders to finally seek help (and why it’s often personal)\n- How Andrew's using AI to scale self-coaching\n- What leaders must unlearn to thrive in the modern world",
          "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jameserichardson_new-podcast-with-andrew-tallents-founder-activity-7338108935574335488-uUA7?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
          "post_type": "regular",
          "author": {
            "first_name": "James",
            "last_name": "Richardson",
            "headline": "Founder of Quantum Growth & Podcast Host | Building Systems to Help Founders Scale at AI-Speed",
            "username": "jameserichardson",
            "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/jameserichardson?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAACsIZZEBPTdvoBEnNbPNX6TuQ_HNYqqXrI8",
            "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4D03AQH5ESnreklcJQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1722422167640?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=iWXB8vSVGUIX-GJoqaT-yeBbXH-9CSACZuhDExUKAjc"
          },
          "stats": {
            "total_reactions": 0,
            "like": 0,
            "support": 0,
            "love": 0,
            "insight": 0,
            "celebrate": 0,
            "funny": 0,
            "comments": 0,
            "reposts": 0
          },
          "article": {
            "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/pulse/hidden-addiction-driving-most-founders-how-breaks-cycle-richardson-ddqcf?trackingId=e6deXOoK1iQ4Uz6nk977lw%3D%3D",
            "title": "The Hidden Addiction Driving Most Founders – And How Self-Coaching Breaks the Cycle",
            "subtitle": "James Richardson",
            "thumbnail": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/D4D12AQFi1l-lwcifGg/article-cover_image-shrink_720_1280/B4DZdQg7WCH4AI-/0/1749402502630?e=1761177600&v=beta&t=zZ5uUq2mkvA2vs4U1OxeLE7hxBM5sIbKVjn9K477mhM"
          }
        }
      },
      {
        "urn": "7337418463457538048",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7337418430926516224",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-08 12:01:24",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749376884331
        },
        "text": "I’ve coached 100s of CEOs & Founders who thought they had no choice.\n\n(At first, they believed pressure and pace were fixed conditions.)\n\nBut every outcome is shaped by the choices you make.\n\nYou choose how to respond when tension rises.\n\nYou choose where to focus your time and energy.\n\nYou choose whether to react or reflect before acting.\n\nThe key? Leadership isn’t about control—it’s about choice.\n\nWant to make better choices? Try this:\n\n1. Pause before defaulting to your usual response.\n\n2. Ask, “What outcome do I want to create here?”\n\n3. Choose with intention—not out of habit or fear.\n\nWhat choices have you already made today?\nAnd what result are they creating?\n\n♻️ Repost to help leaders own their power to choose.\n\nMy name is Andrew Tallents – The Self-Coaching Expert.\n\nWe’re helping leaders succeed through conscious choice.\n\nJoin us here\n https://lnkd.in/ebq4vCHS",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_ive-coached-100s-of-ceos-founders-who-activity-7337418463457538048-VEYI?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

        "media": {
          "type": "video",
          "url": "https://dms.licdn.com/playlist/vid/v2/D4E05AQFbKSjbyybaHw/mp4-720p-30fp-crf28/B4EZdO_H5VHgBQ-/0/1749376879448?e=1756296000&v=beta&t=0Nq6Q0a3LNVGDMUookaeyVWdsztW4pn6Ua4KA37-2cc",

      {
        "urn": "7336841837812346882",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7336841837812346882",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-06 21:50:06",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749239406064
        },
        "text": "The most courageous leaders I know are the ones coaching themselves.\nQuietly. Daily. Relentlessly.\n\nThey don’t wait for permission to grow.\nThey don’t rely on quarterly reviews to course-correct.\nThey lead themselves — before they lead others.\n\n👥 That’s why I’ve launched a private Self-Coaching for Leaders community — a space designed exclusively for CEOs, Founders, and leaders who want to live their personal and professional dreams.\n\nThis is not another leadership forum.\n It’s a practical, brave space to:\n ✅ Learn how to self-coach with purpose and clarity\n ✅ Join weekly “Meet the Expert” sessions with world-class thinkers\n ✅ Stay at the forefront of developments in self-leadership\n ✅ Engage in meaningful, off-the-record conversations with peers\n ✅ Access exclusive tools, insights, and reflections — drawn from my book and podcasts\n\n🧭 If you're leading in a business but want to lead yourself better...\n 🎯 If you're outwardly successful but craving inward alignment...\n 💬 If you’re curious and ready for deeper conversations…\n\nThen this space is for you.\n\nBecause the greatest competitive advantage isn’t strategy.\n\nIt’s self-awareness.\n\nJoin by clicking the link \n\nhttps://lnkd.in/ebq4vCHS\n\n#SelfLeadership #SelfCoaching #FounderCommunity #CEOdevelopment #PersonalGrowth #Leadership",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_selfleadership-selfcoaching-foundercommunity-activity-7336841837812346882-7Vdi?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

      {
        "urn": "7336769367797288961",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7336769367797288961",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-06 17:02:07",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749222127866
        },
        "text": "More from our research into our podcast guests\n\nSuccess doesn’t come from ideas.\nIt comes from the people around you.\n\n75+ leaders have shared their stories with us.\nAnd we’ve heard a consistent thread:\n\n“I kept the wrong people for too long.”\n\nNot because they were bad.\nBut because they were misaligned.\nOr resistant to change.\nOr slowly draining the team’s energy.\n\nLetting people go felt like failure.\nBut keeping them? That was a slow compromise.\n\nKindness is giving honest feedback.\nLeadership is acting on it.\n\nWhat did you learn from keeping someone too long?\nWhat would you do differently now?\n\nComment below\n\n#instaenergy #wrongperson #honestfeedback #podcastguests #research",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_instaenergy-wrongperson-honestfeedback-activity-7336769367797288961--jun?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

      {
        "urn": "7336408182702948353",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7336408182702948353",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-05 17:06:54",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Edited • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749136014629
        },
        "text": "Alvin Foo was our 75th special guest on our Confessions of a Successful Leader podcast today.  To celebrate this landmark we are posting a short series of findings from extensive research into our guests over the last two years.\n\nOne of the hardest leadership lessons?\n \nIt’s not strategy.\nIt’s not vision.\nIt’s learning to delegate.\n\nWe’ve heard this again and again on Confessions of a Successful Leader.\n \nControl and perfectionism got them here. But letting go is what allowed them to grow.\n\nLeaders describe the moment they realised:\n\n\"If I keep doing everything myself, I’ll always be the bottleneck.\"\n\nLearning to trust others.\nLetting go of the idea that your way is the only way.\nBeing okay with ‘good enough’ if it means progress.\n\nIt’s uncomfortable. It’s also essential.\n\nYou can’t scale what you don’t share.\n \nWhich task was hardest for you to delegate?\n\nWhat did others learn because you let them try?",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_alvin-foo-was-our-75th-special-guest-on-our-activity-7336408182702948353-07ye?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },

      {
        "urn": "7336285850562973697",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7336285849203990528",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-05 09:00:48",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749106848374
        },
        "text": "Founders don’t fear change—they evolve with it.\nBut stepping into the corporate world can test even the boldest.\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, Alvin Foo shares how he transformed from scrappy startup founder to boardroom-ready executive—without losing the edge that made him stand out in the first place.\n\n🎙 This episode is a masterclass in reinvention.\n\n💡 Key insights from Alvin's journey:\n➡️ Entrepreneurial DNA belongs in the boardroom: Creative risk-taking doesn’t need to die in a corporate setting.\n➡️ Structure doesn’t mean stagnation: Adapting to systems and processes can amplify—not suppress—your impact.\n➡️ Delegate to grow: True leadership is knowing when to let go and build around you.\n➡️ Control vs. Empowerment: The best leaders don’t micromanage—they inspire trust.\n➡️ Reinvention is a mindset: The future belongs to those willing to keep learning, especially in tech.\n\nAlvin’s story—from founding a voice-over IP company to working at Google and now investing in Web3—is packed with lessons for leaders balancing ambition, adaptability, and authenticity.\n\n🎧 Tune in and learn how to stay bold, stay relevant, and lead with intent.\n\n👀 Watch the full episode: https://lnkd.in/ecMK7qEg\n🎧 Subscribe to Confessions of a Successful Leader YouTube channel: https://lnkd.in/etseyEXe\n🔗 To access all episodes of my podcast, click here: https://lnkd.in/evC2ztmE\n\n\n#founderjourney #corporateleadership #leadershipevolution #selfcoaching #confessionsofasuccessfulleader #web3 #alvinfoo #startupmindset #executivegrowth",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_alvin-foo-confessions-of-a-successful-leader-activity-7336285850562973697-m0s-?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"

      {
        "urn": "7335998800093700096",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7335998800093700096",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-04 14:00:10",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1749038410209
        },
        "text": "I’ve coached Chair–CEO relationships at the highest level.\n(At first, many think alignment means “not rocking the boat.”)\n\nBut real alignment is built on trust, honesty, and challenge.\n\nCoaching the relationship builds shared understanding.\n\nIt surfaces tensions before they derail the Board.\n\nIt ensures decisions are aligned, not just agreed.\n\nThe key? A strong Chair–CEO relationship anchors the Board.\n\nHow aligned are the top two voices in your organisation?\n\n♻️ Repost to highlight why relational coaching matters.\n\nMy name is Andrew Tallents – The Self-Coaching Expert.\n\nWe help Board leaders develop the relationship that drives clarity, confidence, and credibility.\n\nComment “Board” or DM me to learn more.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_ive-coached-chairceo-relationships-at-the-activity-7335998800093700096-oKbn?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",

        "urn": "7335738466095296512",

        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-03 20:45:41",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1748976341747
        },
        "text": "I’ve coached 100s of CEOs and Founders on self-leadership.\n(At first, many believed things were just happening to them.)\n\nBut everything changes when you own your choices.\n\nYou choose how to show up in every relationship.\n\nYou choose what to focus on and what to ignore.\n\nYou choose whether to react or respond with intent.\n\nThe key? Your results reflect your daily choices.\n\nWhat choices have shaped your day so far?\n\n♻️ Repost to help more leaders take ownership today.\n\nMy name is Andrew Tallents – The Self-Coaching Expert.\n\nWe’re helping Founders and CEOs lead themselves first.\n\nComment “mastermind” below or DM me to learn more.\n\n#choices #selfleadership #coaching",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_choices-selfleadership-coaching-activity-7335738466095296512-q34a?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",

      {
        "urn": "7335355962754539522",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7335355961924050944",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-02 19:25:45",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1748885145844
        },
        "text": "Are you contemplating a leap from corporate life to entrepreneurship? 🤔\n\nThe transition can be daunting, but here's a crucial tip from Raghu Kerakatty's journey:\n\nGive yourself time to be absolutely certain about your venture. 🕰️\n\nWhy is this so important?\n\nClarity of purpose becomes your anchor during tough times. It helps you normalize the idea of leaving your steady paycheck. It allows you to plan your finances for the long haul.\n\nRemember, starting a business isn't a sprint; it's a marathon. You need to be prepared to commit for the long term.\n\nRaghu emphasizes: \"Your reason to start needs to be strong enough for you to get through those tough phases.\"\n\nSo, before you take the plunge:\n\nReflect deeply on your motivations. Ensure your purpose aligns with your values. Plan your finances meticulously\n\nBy giving yourself ample time to solidify your purpose, you're not just starting a business - you're embarking on a journey that could redefine your entire career trajectory.\n\nAre you ready to give your entrepreneurial dreams the time they deserve?\n\n#plunge #career #success #paycheck  💡",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_plunge-career-success-activity-7335355962754539522-KjMT?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

          "timestamp": 1748796906105
        },
        "text": "Still waiting for the perfect training programme?\nStill reading books but stuck in the same patterns?\n\nLet’s be honest—most leadership development doesn’t stick.\n\nWhy?\nBecause it’s out of context.\nOut of time.\nAnd out of touch with your real challenges.\n\nI’ve coached 100s of leaders.\nThe breakthrough always comes \"in the moment\".\n\nNot after a webinar.\nNot from a leadership book.\nBut when the pressure hits—and you're alone.\n\nNow you’re not.\n\nOur AI coach in your pocket is here.\n24/7. No ego. No fluff. No excuses.\n\nReal leadership growth happens in real time.\n\n🧠 Reflect.\n💬 Reframe.\n🚀 Respond.\n\nRight where you work.\nRight when you need it.\n\nIf you're serious about evolving as a leader, there's no excuse.\n\nComment \"coach\" or DM me to gain a free trial and VIP onboarding with AI Coach expert Olivier Malafronte. Only 10 VIP passes available.\n\n♻️ Repost to challenge the status quo in leadership.",

      {
        "urn": "7334946404106211328",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7334946404106211328",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-06-01 16:18:19",

          "timestamp": 1748787499453
        },
        "text": "I love this post from Sharon Hulce. The last explanation of what love is should be shared with all the leaders in your network. Love is presence. Presence is space for someone else to just be with you. In this space they are willing to lead or follow. #presence #consciousleadership",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_extraordinarynotordinary-activity-7334946404106211328-8CSf?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "quote",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",
          "profile_picture": "https://media.licdn.com/dms/image/v2/C4E03AQGqgXHYl-XeBQ/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/profile-displayphoto-shrink_800_800/0/1554146172627?e=1758758400&v=beta&t=Sn-JU5arb1vV2-fAJD-yXPjPLpDC5Dv6JMbwi0_HpVg"
        },
        "stats": {
          "total_reactions": 1,
          "like": 0,

          "funny": 0,

        "reshared_post": {
          "urn": "7162074293080612864",
          "posted_at": {
            "date": "2024-02-10 14:26:16",
            "relative": "1 year ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
            "timestamp": 1707571576376
          },
          "text": "A group of professional people posed this question to a group of 4 to 8 year-olds, 'What does love mean?' The answers they got were broader, deeper, and more profound than anyone could have ever imagined! 👇🏻\n\n'When my grandmother got arthritis, she couldn't bend over and paint her toenails anymore... So my grandfather does it for her all the time, even when his hands got arthritis too. That's love.' Rebecca - age 8\n\n'When someone loves you, the way they say your name is different. You just know that your name is safe in their mouth.' Billy - age 4\n\n'Love is when a girl puts on perfume and a boy puts on shaving cologne and they go out and smell each other.' Karl - age 5\n\n'Love is when you go out to eat and give somebody most of your French fries without making them give you any of theirs.' Chrissy - age 6\n\n'Love is what makes you smile when you're tired.' Terri - age 4\n\n'Love is when my mommy makes coffee for my daddy and she takes a sip before giving it to him, to make sure the taste is OK.' Danny - age 8\n\n'Love is what's in the room with you at Christmas if you stop opening presents and just listen.' Bobby - age 7 (Wow!)\n\n'If you want to learn to love better, you should start with a friend who you hate.' Nikka - age 6 (we need a few million more Nikka's on this planet)\n\n'Love is when you tell a guy you like his shirt, then he wears it every day.' Noelle - age 7\n\n'Love is like a little old woman and a little old man who are still friends even after they know each other so well.' Tommy - age 6\n\n'During my piano recital, I was on a stage and I was scared. I looked at all the people watching me and saw my daddy waving and smiling.  He was the only one doing that. I wasn't scared anymore.' Cindy - age 8\n\n'My mommy loves me more than anybody. You don't see anyone else kissing me to sleep at night.' Clare - age 6\n\n'Love is when Mommy gives Daddy the best piece of chicken.' Elaine - age 5\n\n'Love is when Mommy sees Daddy smelly and sweaty and still says he is handsomer than Robert Redford.' Chris - age 7\n\n'Love is when your puppy licks your face even after you left him alone all day.' Mary Ann - age 4\n\n'I know my older sister loves me because she gives me all her old clothes and has to go out and buy new ones.' Lauren - age 4\n\n'When you love somebody, your eyelashes go up and down and little stars come out of you.' (what an image!) Karen - age 7\n\n'You really shouldn't say 'I love you' unless you mean it. But if you mean it, you should say it a lot. People forget.' Jessica - age 8\n\nAnd the final one: The winner was a four year old child whose next door neighbor was an elderly gentleman who had recently lost his wife. Upon seeing the man cry, the little boy went into the old gentleman's yard, climbed onto his lap, and just sat there. When his mother asked what he had said to the neighbor, the little boy said, 'Nothing, I just helped him cry.'\n\n#extraordinarynotordinary",
          "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/sharonhulce_extraordinarynotordinary-activity-7162074293080612864-W17n?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
          "post_type": "regular",
          "author": {
            "first_name": "Sharon",
            "last_name": "Hulce",

            "username": "sharonhulce",

        "urn": "7334511684050448384",

          "date": "2025-05-31 11:30:54",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1748683854115
        },
        "text": "I’ve coached hundreds of leaders—my biggest mistakes?\n\n(At first, I thought success was about innate talent.)\n\nBut in reality, growth comes from consistent effort.\n\nAvoid my mistakes and:\n→ Focus on daily, small improvements.\n→ Reframe failures as learning opportunities.\n→ Surround yourself with people who challenge you.\n\nThe result? Sustained growth and resilience.\n\nMindset, not talent alone, drives growth.\n\nWhat’s one habit that’s helped you grow?\n\n♻️ Repost to help others embrace a growth mindset.",

      {
        "urn": "7334222533270933504",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7334222533270933504",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-05-30 16:21:55",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1748614915197
        },
        "text": "I’ve coached 200+ leaders to turn their desires into tangible goals.\n\n💡All of my clients learn how to get out of their own way!\n\nThey start leading their teams with ease.\n\nThey gain crystal-clear clarity on what they truly want in life.\n\nThey unlock their full potential and maximize their impact every day.\n\nSome CEOs and founders already know that self-leadership is the key.\n\nThey dedicate time to inner work that sharpens focus.\n\nThey reconnect with what truly matters in life.\n\n🎯The results?\nCEOs who hit their bonus targets consistently.\nLeaders who turn their dreams into real financial freedom.\nFounders who work on the business, not just in it.\n\n---------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n♻️ Repost to add value to your network. \n🔔 Follow Andrew Tallents for more on self-coaching & leadership.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_ive-coached-200-leaders-to-turn-their-desires-activity-7334222533270933504-6WFi?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",

      {
        "urn": "7333825059343507457",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:ugcPost:7333825022983131137",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-05-29 14:02:30",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1748520150028
        },
        "text": "Great leaders don’t just drive outcomes - they grow people.\n\nThis week on Confessions of a Successful Leader, I sat down with Sue Musson - leadership expert, NHS Chair, and bestselling author - to explore what it really takes to lead with clarity, courage, and compassion.\n\nTop takeaways from Sue’s journey:\n\n➡️ Self-awareness is strength\n➡️ Empathy builds trust\n➡️ Diverse voices create better outcomes\n➡️ Call out what matters - good and bad\n➡️ Legacy is helping others believe in themselves\n\nSue’s story is a powerful reminder: leadership isn’t about ego - it’s about impact.\n\n👀 Watch the full episode: https://lnkd.in/exb-7Ha8\n\n------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n🎧 Subscribe to Confessions of a Successful Leader YouTube channel: https://lnkd.in/epA9ttWA\n\n🔗 To access all episodes of my podcast, click here: https://lnkd.in/evC2ztmE\n\n🗞 Subscribe to my Self Coaching for Leaders newsletter: https://lnkd.in/gyBQ7UwX\n\n#Leadership #Empathy #Legacy #SelfAwareness #Podcast #ConfessionsOfASuccessfulLeader",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_leadership-empathy-legacy-activity-7333825059343507457-ltkG?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",
          "username": "andrewtallents",
          "profile_url": "https://www.linkedin.com/in/andrewtallents?miniProfileUrn=urn%3Ali%3Afsd_profile%3AACoAAAAUR_wBtdhAz3lgtunyZc48nzgckHZU-DQ",

      {
        "urn": "7333450735222743040",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7333450735222743040",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-05-28 13:15:04",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1748430904203
        },
        "text": "CEOs, are you letting productivity killers sabotage your leadership?\n\n🚫 1. Endless Meetings\nYou attend meetings \"just in case,\" leaving with no clear outcome.\nWhat to do instead: Only join meetings with a clear agenda and decision point. Decline or delegate if not.\n\n🔄 2. Constant Context Switching\nYou’re jumping between tasks, losing focus and energy.\nWhat to do instead: Block out 1-2 hour focus sessions and protect your deep work time.\n\n⚠️ 3. Lack of Focused Priorities\nBy 3pm, you’re exhausted with little to show for it.\nWhat to do instead: Set 1-3 outcomes the night before and focus only on those.\n\n🔥 4. Living in Reactive Mode\nYou’re constantly putting out fires instead of leading.\nWhat to do instead: Start your day with your priorities before distractions take over.\n\n💡 5. Poor Energy Management\nYou’re powering through, but your brain feels drained.\nWhat to do instead: Sleep, move, eat well, and take breaks. They’re leadership essentials, not luxuries.\n\n---------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n♻️ Repost to add value to your network. \n🔔 Follow Andrew Tallents for more on self-coaching & leadership.",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_ceos-are-you-letting-productivity-killers-activity-7333450735222743040-caki?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",

      {
        "urn": "7333195130646007809",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7333195130646007809",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-05-27 20:19:23",

          "timestamp": 1748369963323
        },
        "text": "🌍 80+ Countries and Counting…\n\nWhat started as a spark of an idea…\n…has become a global conversation on leadership, courage, and self-trust.\n\n🎙️ The Confessions of a Successful Leader Podcast has now reached listeners in over 80 countries across the world.\n\nThanks for all your support.",

      {
        "urn": "7333118545288982532",
        "full_urn": "urn:li:activity:7333118545288982532",
        "posted_at": {
          "date": "2025-05-27 15:15:03",
          "relative": "2 months ago • Visible to anyone on or off LinkedIn",
          "timestamp": 1748351703951
        },
        "text": "I've spent over 20 years in leadership development, coaching CEOs and founders around the globe. \n\nHere’s what I wish I knew earlier about creating high-performance teams.\n\n➡️ Trust is the secret ingredient!\nMany leaders underestimate how vital trust is to team dynamics. It’s not just about managing tasks; it’s about fostering relationships where team members feel safe to express their ideas and concerns.\n\n➡️ Clarity beats complexity.\nWhen roles are clear, expectations are set, and everyone knows their contribution matters, teams flourish! Confusion leads to frustration; clarity leads to success!\n\n➡️ Embrace vulnerability!\nAdmitting when you don’t have all the answers can empower your team. It creates a culture of open dialogue, where innovation thrives because people feel encouraged to think outside the box without fear of judgment.\n\n📍 Here’s a quick story from my journey: One client of mine struggled with his team feeling disengaged during meetings.\n\nThey were brilliant but kept holding back! So, we implemented a simple technique: each person had a chance to speak without interruption for two minutes. \n\nThe result? Ideas flowed like never before! This created an environment where everyone felt valued and empowered.\n\n💡 Finally, remember this: building high-performance teams isn’t just about hitting targets; it’s about cultivating an atmosphere where people want to show up every day!\n\nWhat strategies have worked for you in building trust within your teams? \n\nLet’s share insights below!",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_ive-spent-over-20-years-in-leadership-development-activity-7333118545288982532-KAa9?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",
        "post_type": "regular",
        "author": {
          "first_name": "Andrew",
          "last_name": "Tallents",
          "headline": "Advising CEOs & Founders to achieve faster growth by mastering self-leadership | Follow for high-impact leadership & mindset insights",

      {

          "timestamp": 1748261390467
        },
        "text": "Real leadership starts when you stop pretending everyone’s okay.\n\nThis week’s newsletter features Nick Clarke, a stroke survivor turned founder, on how lived experience and vulnerability can reshape the way we lead.\n\n💡 A 4-step model for building human-first systems\n🧠 Lessons in resilience, empathy, and quiet leadership\n🤝 Why care should be built into your company’s core\n\nWhat would change if your leadership made space for what’s really going on?\n\nHit reply and subscribe to my weekly newsletter - Self-Coaching for Leaders.\n------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#Leadership #Founders #HumanFirst #SelfLeadership #ConfessionsOfASuccessfulLeader",
        "url": "https://www.linkedin.com/posts/andrewtallents_leadership-founders-humanfirst-activity-7332739743081775105-wWkj?utm_source=social_share_send&utm_medium=member_desktop_web&rcm=ACoAAF3fAZABh_YAgUbLxFteN8U-zX9j7nhiio0",

      {

          "timestamp": 1748174051790
        },
        "text": "Too Busy to Grow? You’re Not Alone.\n But what if growth could fit into your day?\n\nEvery leader knows the feeling:\n 📅 Back-to-back meetings.\n 📌 Constant demands.\n 💭 No time to pause, let alone reflect.\n\nYet reflection is where the real growth happens.\nThat’s why we’re hosting a free private event at 1500 BST on May 28th.\n It’s not just a webinar—it’s a co-creation session.\n And you’re invited.\n\n🎙 What’s Happening?\nJoin Andrew Tallents (leadership coach + self-coaching expert)\n and Olivier Malafronte (AI coaching innovator)\n for a behind-the-scenes look at the future of self-coaching.\n\nThis is for you if you want to:\nBuild habits of reflection into your day\nDiscover how AI can support real-time decisions\nHelp shape tools that fit your actual leadership life\n\n💡 Why You Should Join:\nBe part of shaping tools made for leaders by leaders\nAccess fresh thinking before anyone else\nSpeak directly with the creators\n\n🔒 Limited Spots. Intimate Format.\nWe’re capping attendance to keep it interactive and personal.\n\n👉 Register Now: https://lnkd.in/enzrTijy\n\n Secure your seat today.\n\nLet’s make time for growth—without needing more time.\n Let’s co-create the next chapter in self-coaching.",

          "timestamp": 1748173954578
        },
        "text": "What a brilliant start to Walkabout Wirral on 22nd May 🌤️\n\nWe walked, we talked, we connected.\n\nInspired by Ben Brabyn's Walkabout London, we launched a Wirral chapter to bring people together for fresh air, open minds, and powerful conversations.\n\nNo agenda. No pressure. Just walking side-by-side and thinking more clearly.\n\nNext Walk: Thursday 26th June at 5pm\n End your day with inspiring company and a scenic route.\n💬 Want to join us? Find out more here \n\nhttps://lnkd.in/erY7fQyG\n\n\nLet’s see where the path takes us.",
